/* BUILD: events-first (v10) */
/* ===== Home scroll position + browser Back button (added) =====
   Opening a section from the Home page (Browse Now, Academic Guide, Persons In Charge, the See All pages) remembers where Home was
   scrolled to and adds ONE browser-history entry. Going back (the arrow on the page, the Home tab, or the browser / phone Back button)
   returns to Home at the same place instead of the top. Everything else keeps working exactly as before. */
window.FIXIT_NAV=(function(){
  var homeY=0, SUB={browse:1,acad:1,persons:1}, hooks={}, timer=0;
  function view(){ var v=document.querySelector('.view.on'); return v?v.id:'home'; }
  function pageY(){ return window.pageYOffset||document.documentElement.scrollTop||0; }
  function tag(){ var s=history.state; return s&&typeof s==='object'&&s.fx?String(s.fx):''; }
  function isOpen(){ return !!(hooks.isOpen&&hooks.isOpen()); }
  function auto(){ try{ history.scrollRestoration='auto'; }catch(e){} }
  function saveHome(){ if(view()==='home') homeY=pageY(); }
  function restoreHome(){
    var y=homeY; window.scrollTo(0,y);
    window.requestAnimationFrame(function(){ if(view()==='home'&&!isOpen()) window.scrollTo(0,y); }); /* once more after the page has finished laying out */
  }
  function paint(id){ /* show one view and light the matching tab (the sections opened from Home belong to the Home tab) */
    var tab=SUB[id]?'home':id;
    document.querySelectorAll('.view').forEach(function(v){ v.classList.toggle('on',v.id===id); });
    document.querySelectorAll('nav button').forEach(function(b){ b.classList.toggle('on',b.dataset.v===tab); });
  }
  function push(name,kind){
    try{ history.scrollRestoration='manual'; var st={fx:name}; if(kind) st.k=kind; history.pushState(st,''); }catch(e){}
  }
  function goHome(){ /* back to Home, same scroll position */
    if(tag()){
      history.back(); clearTimeout(timer);
      timer=setTimeout(function(){ if(view()!=='home'||isOpen()){ if(isOpen()&&hooks.hide) hooks.hide(); paint('home'); restoreHome(); auto(); } },400); /* safety net if the browser did not answer */
      return;
    }
    paint('home'); restoreHome();
  }
  function closeOverlay(){
    if(tag()==='allov'){
      history.back(); clearTimeout(timer);
      timer=setTimeout(function(){ if(isOpen()){ if(hooks.hide) hooks.hide(); restoreHome(); auto(); } },400);
    } else { if(hooks.hide) hooks.hide(); restoreHome(); }
  }
  window.addEventListener('popstate',function(e){ /* the browser / phone Back (or Forward) button */
    var st=e.state, t=st&&typeof st==='object'&&st.fx?String(st.fx):'', open=isOpen();
    if(t==='allov'){ saveHome(); if(!open&&hooks.open) hooks.open(st.k,true); return; }
    if(open&&hooks.hide) hooks.hide();
    if(SUB[t]){ saveHome(); paint(t); window.scrollTo(0,0); return; }
    if(view()!=='home') paint('home');
    restoreHome(); auto();
  });
  try{ if(history.state&&history.state.fx) history.replaceState(null,''); }catch(e){} /* after a page refresh, start clean at Home */
  auto();
  return { view:view, tag:tag, saveHome:saveHome, restoreHome:restoreHome, paint:paint, push:push, goHome:goHome, closeOverlay:closeOverlay, hooks:hooks, isSub:function(id){ return !!SUB[id]; } };
})();
/* Homepage content (Grand Project, Latest in ASHS, Events). Everything on the homepage in these three sections comes from
   this list, so the admin can add, edit and remove every entry. The starting entries below are the original samples. */

  var DEF={ grand: [], latest: [], events: [] }; /* nothing is built in: the homepage shows only what the admin adds */
  /* default Grand Project picture (embedded, so no separate file is needed) */

  var BUILTIN=/^d-[glev]\d+$/; /* ids of the old built-in sample entries; they are removed wherever they are found */
  /* Latest in ASHS cards keep their normal size even when only 1 or 2 are uploaded (they no longer stretch across the row),
     and Latest / Events show a friendly card when nothing has been added yet. */
  (function(){
    var st=document.createElement('style');
    st.textContent='@media (min-width:900px){.hscroll .reno{max-width:calc((100% - 48px)/3)}}'+
      '.emp{flex:1 1 100%;min-width:0;scroll-snap-align:start;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;text-align:center;gap:6px;padding:34px 20px;background:linear-gradient(180deg,#fff,var(--tint));border:2px dashed var(--line);border-radius:20px;color:var(--mute)}'+
      '.emp__ic{width:64px;height:64px;border-radius:50%;background:#fff;color:var(--brand);display:grid;place-items:center;margin-bottom:6px;box-shadow:0 6px 16px rgba(21,83,214,.15)}'+
      '.emp__ic svg{width:30px;height:30px}.emp b{color:var(--ink);font-size:18px}.emp__t{font-size:14px;max-width:360px;line-height:1.45}';
    st.textContent+='.allov{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:2000;background:var(--bg);overflow-y:auto}.allov[hidden]{display:none!important}'+
      '.allov__bar{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:2;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:14px 16px;background:var(--bg);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}'+
      '.allov__bar h2{margin:0;font-size:20px;color:var(--ink)}.allov__back{width:42px;height:42px;border:0;border-radius:50%;background:var(--brand);color:#fff;font-size:22px;line-height:1;cursor:pointer}'+
      '.allov__body{max-width:1040px;margin:0 auto;padding:18px 16px 60px}.allov__grid{display:grid;gap:20px;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(280px,1fr))}'+
      '.allov__grid.is-events{grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(320px,1fr))}.allov__h{margin:26px 0 12px;font-size:20px;color:var(--ink);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}.allov__h:first-child{margin-top:6px}.allov__h::before{content:"";width:5px;height:22px;border-radius:3px;background:var(--gold)}'+
      '.allov .reno,.allov .evc{flex:none;width:auto;max-width:none}';
    /* Events header: the arrows and "See All" sit in one neat row */
    st.textContent+='.sh .ctl{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}.sh .ctl .arr{margin:0;width:42px;height:42px;transition:background .15s,color .15s,transform .15s,box-shadow .15s}'+
      '.sh .ctl .arr:hover{background:var(--brand);color:#fff;transform:translateY(-1px)}.sh .ctl .arr:active{transform:scale(.94)}'+
      '@media (max-width:560px){.sh .ctl{gap:8px}.sh .ctl .arr{width:38px;height:38px}}';
    document.head.appendChild(st);
  })();
  var EMP_ICONS={
    news:'<path d="M5 5h11v14H7a2 2 0 0 1-2-2z"/><path d="M16 9h3v8a2 2 0 0 1-2 2"/><path d="M8.5 9h4M8.5 12h4M8.5 15h2.5"/>',
    cal:'<rect x="4" y="5.5" width="16" height="14" rx="2"/><path d="M4 10h16M8.5 3.5v4M15.5 3.5v4"/>'
  };
  function emp(icon,title,text){ return '<div class="emp"><span class="emp__ic"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">'+EMP_ICONS[icon]+'</svg></span><b>'+title+'</b><span class="emp__t">'+text+'</span></div>'; }
  var mem=null;
  function save(c){ mem=c; var ok=false; try{ localStorage.setItem('fixit.content',JSON.stringify(c)); ok=true; }catch(e){} try{ if(window.FIXIT_SYNC) window.FIXIT_SYNC.content(c); }catch(e){} return ok; }
  function get(){
    var c=null; try{ c=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fixit.content')||'null'); }catch(e){}
    if(!c&&mem) c=mem;
    if(!c||!c.seeded){ /* first run (or older saved content): start from the original samples, keep anything already added */
      var old=c||{}; c={seeded:1};
      ['grand','latest','events'].forEach(function(t){ c[t]=DEF[t].map(function(x){return Object.assign({},x);}).concat(old[t]||[]); });
      save(c);
    }
    var cleaned=false;
    ['grand','latest','events'].forEach(function(t){ if(!c[t]) c[t]=[]; var n=c[t].length; c[t]=c[t].filter(function(x){ return !(x&&BUILTIN.test(String(x.id))); }); if(c[t].length!==n) cleaned=true; });
    if(cleaned){ mem=c; try{ localStorage.setItem('fixit.content',JSON.stringify(c)); }catch(e){} }
    return c;
  }
  var esc=function(s){var d=document.createElement('div');d.textContent=s==null?'':String(s);return d.innerHTML};
  function ph(x,i,label){
    var img=x.img&&/^data:image\/(jpeg|png|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+\/=]+$/.test(x.img)?x.img:'';
    return '<div class="ph p'+(i%3+1)+'">'+(img?'<img src="'+img+'" alt="">':'<svg><use href="#cam"/></svg><span>'+label+'</span>')+'</div>';
  }
  function pd(d){ return d?new Date(d+'T00:00'):null; }
  /* How many entries the homepage shows. The rest are opened with "See All". */
  var MAX_LATEST=3, MAX_EVENTS=4;
  function sortedLatest(c){ return c.latest.slice().sort(function(a,b){ return (b.date||'').localeCompare(a.date||''); }); } /* newest first */
  function sortedEvents(c){ return c.events.slice().sort(function(a,b){ if(!a.date) return 1; if(!b.date) return -1; return b.date.localeCompare(a.date); }); } /* the latest dates first */
  function byDateAsc(a,b){ if(!a.date) return 1; if(!b.date) return -1; return a.date.localeCompare(b.date); }
  function todayStr(){ var d=new Date(); return d.getFullYear()+'-'+('0'+(d.getMonth()+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+d.getDate()).slice(-2); }
  function homeEvents(c){
    var t0=todayStr(), list=(c&&Array.isArray(c.events)?c.events:[]).filter(function(x){ return x&&typeof x==='object'; }),
        dated=list.filter(function(x){ return typeof x.date==='string'&&x.date; }), undated=list.filter(function(x){ return !(typeof x.date==='string'&&x.date); }),
        upc=dated.filter(function(x){ return x.date>=t0; }).sort(byDateAsc), past=dated.filter(function(x){ return x.date<t0; }).sort(function(a,b){ return b.date.localeCompare(a.date); });
    return upc.concat(undated).slice(0,MAX_EVENTS); /* past events are never on the homepage; they are only under See All */
  }
  /* Latest in ASHS on the homepage: entries dated before today are not shown (they stay under See All), newest first */
  /* Grand Project showcase: ONLY current or upcoming entries (no date, or dated today or later). Past ones stay under See All. */
  function grandCurrent(c){ var t0=todayStr(); return (c&&Array.isArray(c.grand)?c.grand:[]).filter(function(x){ return x&&typeof x==='object'&&!(typeof x.date==='string'&&x.date&&x.date<t0); }); }
  function homeLatest(c){ var t0=todayStr(); return sortedLatest(c).filter(function(x){ return !x.date||x.date>=t0; }).slice(0,MAX_LATEST); }
  function latestCard(x,i){
    var d=pd(x.date), ds=d&&!isNaN(d)?d.toLocaleDateString('en',{month:'short',day:'numeric',year:'numeric'}):'';
    return '<article class="card reno">'+ph(x,i,'Photo')+'<div class="t"><small>'+esc(ds)+'</small><b>'+esc(x.title)+'</b><p>'+esc(x.text||'')+'</p></div></article>';
  }
  function eventCard(x){
    var d=pd(x.date); if(d&&isNaN(d)) d=null;
    return '<article class="card evc"><div class="date">'+(d?('0'+d.getDate()).slice(-2):'--')+'<small>'+(d?d.toLocaleString('en',{month:'short'}).toUpperCase():'')+'</small></div><div><b>'+esc(x.title)+'</b><span class="mute">'+esc(x.text||'')+'</span></div></article>';
  }
  /* "See All": a full page listing every entry */
  function openAll(kind,fromPop){
    var c=get(), ov=document.getElementById('allov');
    if(!ov){
      ov=document.createElement('div'); ov.id='allov'; ov.className='allov'; ov.setAttribute('role','dialog'); ov.setAttribute('aria-modal','true'); ov.hidden=true;
      ov.innerHTML='<div class="allov__bar"><button type="button" class="allov__back" aria-label="Back">&larr;</button><h2 id="allov-t"></h2></div><div class="allov__body" id="allov-b"></div>';
      document.body.appendChild(ov);
      ov.querySelector('.allov__back').addEventListener('click',closeAll);
    }
    var body=ov.querySelector('#allov-b'), html='';
    function sec(title,cls,cards){ return cards.length?'<h3 class="allov__h">'+title+'</h3><div class="allov__grid '+cls+'">'+cards.join('')+'</div>':''; }
    if(kind==='events'){ /* upcoming (today or later, soonest first) and past (newest first), like the "What's On" page */
      var t=todayStr(), all=sortedEvents(c);
      var up=all.filter(function(x){ return !x.date||x.date>=t; }).sort(byDateAsc), past=all.filter(function(x){ return x.date&&x.date<t; });
      html=sec('Upcoming Events','is-events',up.map(eventCard))+sec('Past Events','is-events',past.map(eventCard));
      ov.querySelector('#allov-t').textContent='All Events';
    } else if(kind==='grand'){ /* every uploaded Grand Project entry: current and upcoming first (soonest first), then the past ones (newest first) */
      var gt=todayStr(), gl=(c.grand||[]).filter(function(x){ return x&&typeof x==='object'; });
      var gUp=gl.filter(function(x){ return !(typeof x.date==='string'&&x.date)||x.date>=gt; }).sort(byDateAsc),
          gPast=gl.filter(function(x){ return typeof x.date==='string'&&x.date&&x.date<gt; }).sort(function(a,b){ return b.date.localeCompare(a.date); });
      html=sec('Current &amp; Upcoming','is-latest',gUp.map(function(x,i){ return latestCard(x,i); }))+sec('Past Events','is-latest',gPast.map(function(x,i){ return latestCard(x,i+gUp.length); }));
      ov.querySelector('#allov-t').textContent='All Grand Projects';
    } else { /* the newest entries (the ones on the homepage) and the older ones below them */
      var lat=sortedLatest(c), onHome=homeLatest(c), rest=lat.filter(function(x){ return onHome.indexOf(x)<0; });
      html=sec('Latest','is-latest',onHome.map(latestCard))+sec('Past','is-latest',rest.map(function(x,i){ return latestCard(x,i+onHome.length); }));
      ov.querySelector('#allov-t').textContent='All Latest in ASHS';
    }
    body.innerHTML=html||'<p class="note">Nothing here yet.</p>';
    if(!fromPop&&window.FIXIT_NAV){ window.FIXIT_NAV.saveHome(); window.FIXIT_NAV.push('allov',kind); } /* the Back button closes this page */
    ov.hidden=false; ov.scrollTop=0; document.body.style.overflow='hidden';
  }
  function hideAll(){ var ov=document.getElementById('allov'); if(ov) ov.hidden=true; document.body.style.overflow=''; }
  function allOpen(){ var ov=document.getElementById('allov'); return !!ov&&!ov.hidden; }
  function closeAll(){ if(!allOpen()) return; if(window.FIXIT_NAV) window.FIXIT_NAV.closeOverlay(); else hideAll(); } /* closing returns to the same place on Home */
  if(window.FIXIT_NAV){ window.FIXIT_NAV.hooks.open=openAll; window.FIXIT_NAV.hooks.hide=hideAll; window.FIXIT_NAV.hooks.isOpen=allOpen; }
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var a=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#latestAll,#eventsAll,#grandAll'); if(!a) return;
    e.preventDefault(); openAll(a.id==='eventsAll'?'events':a.id==='grandAll'?'grand':'latest');
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){ if(e.key==='Escape') closeAll(); });
  function render(){
    var c=get(), $=function(s){return document.querySelector(s)};
    var g=$('#grandSlides'), dots=$('#home .dots'), hs=$('#home .hscroll'), ev=$('#evs');
    /* Events first, in its own try: a problem in another section can never stop the events from showing what is saved in db.json */
    try{
    if(ev){
      /* Events: only what is saved in db.json. The homepage carries up to 4 UPCOMING events (today or later), nearest date first; the < > arrows move 2 at a time and See All always shows every upcoming and past event. */
      var shown=homeEvents(c);
      ev.innerHTML=shown.length?shown.map(eventCard).join(''):emp('cal','No upcoming events','Upcoming school events will be listed here once they are added.');
      ev.scrollLeft=0;
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-for="evs"]').forEach(function(b){ b.style.display=shown.length?'':'none'; /* arrows appear as soon as there is 1 upcoming event */ });
      var ctl=ev.previousElementSibling&&ev.previousElementSibling.querySelector('.ctl');
      if(ctl){ var ea=$('#eventsAll'); if(!ea){ ea=document.createElement('a'); ea.id='eventsAll'; ea.className='see'; ea.href='#'; ea.innerHTML='See All <svg><use href="#chev"/></svg>'; ctl.appendChild(ea); } ea.style.display=(c.events||[]).length?'':'none'; /* See All appears as soon as any event exists */ }
    }
    }catch(err){ if(window.console) console.error('FIXIT events',err); try{ ev.innerHTML=emp('cal','Events could not load','Press Ctrl+F5. If it stays like this, press F12 and read the FIXIT events message in the Console.'); }catch(e2){} }
    try{
    if(g){
      /* default picture: shown when nothing is current (no entries, or every entry is dated before today) */
      var gCur=grandCurrent(c), gActive=gCur.length>0;
      g.innerHTML=gActive?gCur.map(function(x,i){
        return '<figure class="slide">'+ph(x,i,'Photo of completed project')+'<figcaption><em>Completed</em><b>'+esc(x.title)+'</b><small>'+esc(x.text||'School-wide project')+'</small></figcaption></figure>';
      }).join(''):'<figure class="slide slide--default"><img src="'+DEFAULT_GRAND+'" alt="Grand Project" onerror="this.parentNode.innerHTML=\'<div class=&quot;ph p1&quot;><svg><use href=&quot;#cam&quot;/></svg><span>No grand projects yet</span></div>\'"></figure>';
      g.scrollLeft=0; if(g.parentNode) g.parentNode.classList.toggle('grand-default',!gActive); /* arrows and dots are hidden while the default picture is showing */
      /* "See All" for the Grand Project: visible as soon as ANY entry was uploaded (even when every one is already in the past) */
      var gh=g.parentNode&&g.parentNode.previousElementSibling;
      if(gh&&gh.classList.contains('sh')){ var ga=document.getElementById('grandAll'); if(!ga){ ga=document.createElement('a'); ga.id='grandAll'; ga.className='see'; ga.href='#'; ga.innerHTML='See All <svg><use href="#chev"/></svg>'; gh.appendChild(ga); } ga.style.display=(c.grand||[]).length?'':'none'; }
    }
    if(dots) dots.innerHTML=(gActive?gCur:[0]).map(function(_,i){return '<i'+(i?'':' class="on"')+'></i>';}).join('');
    }catch(err){ if(window.console) console.error('FIXIT grand',err); }
    try{
    if(hs){
      var lat=sortedLatest(c);
      var shownL=homeLatest(c);
      hs.innerHTML=shownL.length?shownL.map(latestCard).join(''):emp('news','No updates yet','News about repairs and school improvements will appear here.');
      var la=$('#home .sh .see:not(#grandAll)'); if(la){ la.id='latestAll'; la.style.display=lat.length?'':'none'; /* See All appears as soon as 1 update is added */ }
    }
    }catch(err){ if(window.console) console.error('FIXIT latest',err); }
  }
  window.FIXIT_HOME={get:get,save:save,render:render};
  render();
})();

  const N=window.FIXIT_NAV,from=N.view();
  if(b.dataset.v==='home'&&from!=='home'&&e&&e.isTrusted){N.goHome();return} /* tapping Home after visiting another page: back to the same place on Home */
  if(from==='home')N.saveHome();
  document.querySelectorAll('nav button').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('on',x===b));
  document.querySelectorAll('.view').forEach(v=>v.classList.toggle('on',v.id===b.dataset.v));
  scrollTo(0,0);
});

  const s=document.getElementById(b.dataset.for);
  if(s.id==='evs'){ /* events move 2 cards at a time (1 on a phone) */
    const c=s.querySelector('.evc'); if(c){ const gap=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(s).columnGap)||14, n=s.clientWidth>=700?2:1; s.scrollBy({left:b.dataset.dir*n*(c.offsetWidth+gap),behavior:'smooth'}); return; }
  }
  s.scrollBy({left:b.dataset.dir*s.clientWidth*.9,behavior:'smooth'});
});

/* ===== Services: search, service picker, selection (added) ===== */
(()=>{
const $=s=>document.querySelector(s),$$=s=>[...document.querySelectorAll(s)];
const list=$('#svcList');if(!list)return;
const inp=$('#svcSearch'),stat=$('#svcStat'),none=$('#svcNone'),all=$('#svcAll'),svc=$('#services');
const norm=s=>s.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim();
const goTo=v=>{const b=$('nav button[data-v="'+v+'"]');if(b)b.click()};
let active=null;
const cats=$$('#svcList .svc').map(el=>{
  const stitle=el.querySelector('h2').textContent,sdesc=el.querySelector('.mute').textContent;
  const items=[...el.querySelectorAll('.item')].map(it=>{
    const iname=it.querySelector('summary b').textContent;
    const grps=[...it.querySelectorAll('.grp')].map(g=>{
      const gname=g.querySelector('b').textContent;
      const chips=[...g.querySelectorAll('.chip')].map(c=>({el:c,hay:norm([el.dataset.k,stitle,sdesc,iname,gname,c.textContent].join(' ')),nar:norm([iname,gname,c.textContent].join(' '))}));
      return{el:g,chips};
    });
    return{el:it,grps};
  });
  return{el,items,i:+el.dataset.i};
});
function run(){
  const tokens=norm(inp.value).split(' ').filter(Boolean);
  let n=0;
  /* match the specific category first (e.g. "trash bin" opens only Waste Management); fall back to the whole service (e.g. "furniture") */
  const qC=tokens.join(''),m=h=>tokens.every(x=>h.includes(x))||h.replace(/ /g,'').includes(qC);
  const narrow=tokens.length>0&&cats.some(c=>(active===null||c.i===active)&&c.items.some(it=>it.grps.some(g=>g.chips.some(ch=>m(ch.nar)))));
  const test=ch=>m(narrow?ch.nar:ch.hay);
  cats.forEach(c=>{let cv=false;
    const inScope=active===null||c.i===active;
    c.items.forEach(it=>{let iv=false;
      it.grps.forEach(g=>{let gv=false;
        g.chips.forEach(ch=>{const ok=inScope&&(!tokens.length||test(ch));ch.el.hidden=!ok;if(ok){gv=true;n++}});
        g.el.hidden=!gv;if(gv)iv=true});
      it.el.hidden=!iv;if(tokens.length)it.el.open=iv;else if(!iv)it.el.open=false;if(iv)cv=true});
    c.el.hidden=!cv});
  $$('.sp').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('on',+b.dataset.s===active));
  all.hidden=active===null&&!tokens.length;
  none.hidden=!(tokens.length&&n===0);
  stat.textContent=tokens.length&&n?n+' matching '+(n===1?'option':'options')+' found':'';
}
const reveal=()=>list.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});
inp.addEventListener('input',()=>{active=null;run()});
$('#svcGo').addEventListener('click',()=>{run();reveal()});
inp.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();run();reveal()}});
$$('.sugg button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{inp.value=b.dataset.q;active=null;run();reveal()}));
$$('.sp').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{const i=+b.dataset.s;active=active===i?null:i;inp.value='';run();if(active!==null)reveal()}));
all.addEventListener('click',()=>{active=null;inp.value='';run()});
/* tapping an option records the selection and offers to report it */
const bar=$('#selBar'),selTxt=$('#selTxt'),selSub=$('#selSub');
list.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const c=e.target.closest('.chip');if(!c)return;
  $$('#svcList .chip.sel').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('sel'));c.classList.add('sel');
  const who=window.FIXIT_API&&window.FIXIT_API.me&&window.FIXIT_API.me();
  if(who&&who.role==='admin'){svc.classList.remove('hasSel');window.FIXIT_SELECTION=null;return}   /* admin: read only, nothing to report */
  const sel={
    service:c.closest('.svc').querySelector('h2').textContent,
    category:c.closest('.item').querySelector('summary b').textContent,
    group:c.closest('.grp').querySelector('b').textContent,
    issue:c.textContent};
  window.FIXIT_SELECTION=sel;
  document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('fixit:select',{detail:sel}));
  selTxt.textContent=sel.category+' › '+sel.issue;
  selSub.textContent=sel.service+' · '+sel.group;
  svc.classList.add('hasSel');
});
$('#selGo').addEventListener('click',()=>goTo('report'));
$('#selX').addEventListener('click',()=>{svc.classList.remove('hasSel');window.FIXIT_SELECTION=null;$$('#svcList .chip.sel').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('sel'))});
/* logout or a finished report: empty the search box, filters and picked issue */
const resetAll=()=>{inp.value='';const hs=$('#home .search input');if(hs)hs.value='';active=null;run();$$('#svcList .item').forEach(d=>d.open=false);$('#selX').click()};
document.addEventListener('fixit:clear-search',resetAll);
/* leaving Services for Home / Activity / Profile puts it back to the original */
$$('nav button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(b.dataset.v!=='services'&&b.dataset.v!=='report')resetAll()}));
/* home page search bar sends the user to Services with the same keywords */
const hi=$('#home .search input'),hb=$('#home .search button');
if(hi&&hb){
  const send=()=>{inp.value=hi.value;active=null;goTo('services');run()};
  hb.addEventListener('click',send);
  hi.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();send()}});
}
})();

/* ===== REPORT AN ISSUE (students + teachers) =====
   One function powers both forms. Element ids are prefixed:
   st-  = student form,  tc-  = teacher form.
   The issue picked on the Services page arrives in window.FIXIT_SELECTION. */
(function () {
  // Student list: Grade -> Building -> Floor -> Areas (Canteen is a direct choice)
  var studentData = {
    "11": {

      },

        "2nd Floor": ["Fibonacci", "Laplace", "Leibniz", "Newton", "Women's Comfort Room", "Men's Comfort Room"],
        "3rd Floor": ["Pearson", "Pascal", "Shakespeare", "Mencius", "Women's Comfort Room", "Men's Comfort Room"],

      },

    }
  };

  // Teacher list: Building -> Floor -> Areas (Canteen is a direct choice)
  var teacherData = {
    "DepEd 1 Building": {
      "1st Floor": ["Descartes", "Einstein", "Euclid", "Euler", "Women's Comfort Room", "Men's Comfort Room"],
      "2nd Floor": ["Fibonacci", "Laplace", "Leibniz", "Newton", "Women's Comfort Room", "Men's Comfort Room"],
      "3rd Floor": ["Pearson", "Pascal", "Shakespeare", "Mencius", "Women's Comfort Room", "Men's Comfort Room"],

    },
    "DepEd 2 Building": {
      "1st Floor": ["Analysts", "Ambassadors", "Advocates", "Women's Comfort Room", "Men's Comfort Room"],
      "2nd Floor": ["Aspirers", "Builders", "Catalysts", "Women's Comfort Room", "Men's Comfort Room"],
      "3rd Floor": ["Voyagers", "Designers", "Conquerors", "Women's Comfort Room", "Men's Comfort Room"],
      "4th Floor": ["Diplomats", "Empiricists", "Strategists", "Women's Comfort Room", "Men's Comfort Room"]
    },
    "EBD Building": {
      "1st Floor": ["Care Center", "School Clinic", "Innovators", "Practitioners", "Pathfinders", "Luminaries", "Women's Comfort Room", "Men's Comfort Room"],
      "2nd Floor": ["Principal's Office", "Admin Office", "Faculty", "School Library", "Elton Mayo", "Philip Crosby", "Women's Comfort Room", "Men's Comfort Room"],
      "3rd Floor": ["Developers", "Thinkers", "Douglas McGregor", "Luca Pacioli", "School Property/Archived", "Women's Comfort Room", "Men's Comfort Room"]
    },

  // ----- Type of Problem list: read straight from the Services page, so the two
  // always match:  Service -> Category -> Issue type -> Option  (+ "Others") -----
  function buildTypeTree() {
    var tree = {};
    document.querySelectorAll("#svcList .svc").forEach(function (svc) {
      var service = svc.querySelector("h2").textContent.trim();
      tree[service] = {};
      svc.querySelectorAll(".item").forEach(function (item) {
        var category = item.querySelector("summary b").textContent.trim();
        tree[service][category] = {};
        item.querySelectorAll(".grp").forEach(function (grp) {
          var group = grp.querySelector("b").textContent.trim();
          tree[service][category][group] = Array.prototype.map.call(
            grp.querySelectorAll(".chip"), function (c) { return c.textContent.trim(); });
        });
      });
    });
    tree["Others"] = null;                 // direct choice, asks "Please specify"
    return tree;
  }
  var typeTree = buildTypeTree();

  // ----- Reusable drill-down dropdown (one box that goes deeper step by step) -----
  var allDrills = [];
  function createDrill(o) {
    var path = [];

    function node() {
      var n = o.getTree();
      path.forEach(function (k) { n = n[k]; });
      return n;
    }

    function addItem(text, drill, onPick) {
      var li = document.createElement("li");
      var b = document.createElement("button");
      b.type = "button";
      b.className = "combo__item";
      b.innerHTML = "<span></span>" + (drill ? '<span class="combo__arrow" aria-hidden="true">&rsaquo;</span>' : "");
      b.firstChild.textContent = text;
      b.addEventListener("click", onPick);
      li.appendChild(b);
      o.list.appendChild(li);
    }

    // Shows the current level: first choices, or the next level down after a pick
    function render() {
      o.list.innerHTML = "";
      o.head.hidden = !path.length;
      o.crumb.textContent = path.join(" \u203A ");
      var n = node();
      if (Array.isArray(n)) {
        n.forEach(function (item) {
          addItem(item, false, function () { choose(path.concat(item)); });
        });
      } else {
        Object.keys(n).forEach(function (key) {
          var direct = n[key] === null;
          addItem(key, !direct, function () {
            if (direct) { choose(path.concat(key)); } else { path.push(key); render(); }
          });
        });
      }
    }

    function choose(full) {
      var text = o.display(full);
      o.hidden.value = text;
      o.valueEl.textContent = text;
      o.valueEl.classList.remove("combo__value--placeholder");
      close();
      if (o.onPick) { o.onPick(full, text); }
    }

    function open() {
      allDrills.forEach(function (d) { if (d !== api) { d.close(); } });   // one open at a time
      path = [];
      render();
      o.panel.hidden = false;
      o.button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
    }

    function close() {
      o.panel.hidden = true;
      o.button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
    }

    function reset(placeholder) {
      o.hidden.value = "";
      o.valueEl.textContent = placeholder;
      o.valueEl.classList.add("combo__value--placeholder");
      close();
    }

    o.button.addEventListener("click", function () { if (o.panel.hidden) { open(); } else { close(); } });
    o.back.addEventListener("click", function () { path.pop(); render(); });

    // Clicks inside the dropdown never close it. (Items are rebuilt on every
    // click, so checking e.target after the rebuild would wrongly close it.)
    o.combo.addEventListener("click", function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });
    document.addEventListener("click", close);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && !o.panel.hidden) { close(); o.button.focus(); }
    });

    // Show the browser's "please fill out this field" on the visible button
    o.hidden.addEventListener("focus", function () { o.button.focus(); });

    var api = { choose: choose, close: close, reset: reset };
    allDrills.push(api);
    return api;
  }

  function initReportForm(P, cfg) {
    function $(id) { return document.getElementById(P + "-" + id); }

    var data = cfg.data || null;          // students get it after picking a grade
    var grade = $("grade");               // only exists on the student form

    // ---- Building & Area:  Building > Floor > Area ----
    var building = createDrill({
      combo: $("combo"), button: $("bs-button"), valueEl: $("bs-value"), panel: $("bs-panel"),
      head: $("bs-head"), back: $("bs-back"), crumb: $("bs-crumb"), list: $("bs-list"),
      hidden: $("building-section"),
      getTree: function () { return data; },
      display: function (p) { return p.length === 3 ? p[0] + ", " + p[1] + " \u2014 " + p[2] : p.join(" \u203A "); }
    });

    // Students: Grade Level decides which Buildings (and areas) can be chosen
    if (grade) {
      grade.addEventListener("change", function () {
        data = cfg.dataByGrade[grade.value];
        building.reset("Select Building & Area");
        $("bs-button").disabled = false;
      });
    }

    // ---- Type of Problem:  Service > Category > Issue type > Option ----
    var otherField = $("other-field");
    var otherInput = $("other-problem");
    var issueKeys = ["issue-service", "issue-category", "issue-group", "issue-detail"];

    var type = createDrill({
      combo: $("type-combo"), button: $("type-button"), valueEl: $("type-value"), panel: $("type-panel"),
      head: $("type-head"), back: $("type-back"), crumb: $("type-crumb"), list: $("type-list"),
      hidden: $("problem-type"),
      getTree: function () { return typeTree; },
      // shown without the service name (the category already says it), e.g. Chairs > Physical Damage > Broken/Cracked
      display: function (p) { return p.length === 1 ? p[0] : p.slice(1).join(" \u203A "); },
      onPick: function (p) {
        var isOther = p.length === 1;                 // "Others" -> ask to specify
        otherField.hidden = !isOther;
        otherInput.required = isOther;
        if (isOther) { otherInput.focus(); } else { otherInput.value = ""; }
        issueKeys.forEach(function (k, i) { $(k).value = isOther ? "" : p[i]; });
      }
    });

    // Photo file name feedback
    var photo = $("photo");
    var photoText = $("photo-text");
    var photoData = "", photoBusy = false;
    photo.addEventListener("change", function () {
      photoData = ""; photoBusy = false;
      if (!photo.files.length) { photoText.textContent = "Take/Upload Photo"; return; }
      var f = photo.files[0];
      photoText.textContent = f.name;
      if (!/^image\//.test(f.type)) { photo.value = ""; photoText.textContent = "Take/Upload Photo"; toast("Please choose a photo (image file)."); return; }
      photoBusy = true;
      shrinkPhoto(f, function (d) {
        photoBusy = false; photoData = d || "";
        if (!d) { photo.value = ""; photoText.textContent = "Take/Upload Photo"; toast("That photo could not be read. Please try another one."); }
      });
    });

    // Submit: the page-level handler (submitReport) sends it; errors show under the form
    var form = $("form"), msg = $("form-message");
    form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      $("datetime").value = new Date().toLocaleString("en-PH", { dateStyle: "long", timeStyle: "short" });
      var res = cfg.onSubmit(form);
      if (!res.ok) { msg.hidden = false; msg.className = "form-message fx-err"; msg.textContent = res.error; }
    });

    return {
      getPhoto: function () { return photoData; },
      photoBusy: function () { return photoBusy; },
      // issue picked on the Services page -> fills Type of Problem
      applyIssue: function (sel) {
        type.choose([sel.service, sel.category, sel.group, sel.issue]);
      },
      clearIssue: function () { issueKeys.forEach(function (k) { $(k).value = ""; }); },
      // wipe the whole form so it is ready for the next report
      reset: function () {
        form.reset(); msg.hidden = true;
        type.reset("Select Type");
        building.reset(grade ? "Select Grade Level first" : "Select Building & Area");
        if (grade) { data = null; $("bs-button").disabled = true; }
        otherField.hidden = true; otherInput.required = false;
        photoText.textContent = "Take/Upload Photo"; photoData = ""; photoBusy = false;
        $("who").hidden = true;
        ["name", "adviser", "section", "grade"].forEach(function (k) { if ($(k)) { $(k).closest(".field").hidden = false; } });
      },
      // fill the details from the logged-in account (so nobody retypes them or reports as someone else)
      fill: function (u) {
        $("name").value = u.name; $("name").closest(".field").hidden = true;
        if (grade) {
          $("adviser").value = u.adviser; $("adviser").closest(".field").hidden = true;
          grade.value = u.grade; grade.dispatchEvent(new Event("change")); grade.closest(".field").hidden = true;
          $("section").value = u.section; $("section").closest(".field").hidden = true;
        }
        $("who-name").textContent = u.name;
        $("who-sub").textContent = grade ? "Grade " + u.grade + " \u00B7 " + u.section + " \u00B7 Adviser: " + u.adviser
          : (u.advisory ? "Teacher \u00B7 Adviser of " + u.section : "Teacher");
        $("who").hidden = false;
      }
    };
  }

  // shrink a chosen photo to a small JPEG so it can be sent and stored quickly
  function shrinkPhoto(file, done) {
    var rd = new FileReader();
    rd.onerror = function () { done(""); };
    rd.onload = function () {
      var im = new Image();
      im.onerror = function () { done(""); };
      im.onload = function () {
        var k = Math.min(1, 1000 / Math.max(im.width, im.height)), cv = document.createElement("canvas");
        cv.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(im.width * k)); cv.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(im.height * k));
        var cx = cv.getContext("2d"); cx.fillStyle = "#fff"; cx.fillRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height); cx.drawImage(im, 0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);
        var q = 0.72, out = cv.toDataURL("image/jpeg", q);
        while (out.length > 380000 && q > 0.3) { q -= 0.1; out = cv.toDataURL("image/jpeg", q); }
        done(out.length > 580000 ? "" : out);
      };
      im.src = rd.result;
    };
    rd.readAsDataURL(file);
  }
  function toast(m) {
    var t = document.getElementById("toast"); if (!t) return;
    t.textContent = m; t.classList.add("on"); setTimeout(function () { t.classList.remove("on"); }, 3200);
  }
  // sends the form to the FIXIT API; on success the form is cleared and we go back to "Who is reporting?"
  function submitReport(role) {
    return function (f) {
      var api = window.FIXIT_API; if (!api) return { ok: false, error: "The page is still loading. Please try again." };
      var fd = new FormData(f), v = function (k) { return String(fd.get(k) || "").trim(); };
      if (forms[role].photoBusy()) return { ok: false, error: "Your photo is still being prepared. Please press Send again in a moment." };
      var res = api.submit({
        photo: forms[role].getPhoto(),
        role: role, name: v("name"), adviser: v("adviser"), section: v("section"), location: v("building_section"),
        category: [v("issue_service"), v("issue_category")].filter(Boolean).join(" \u203A "),
        title: [v("problem_type"), v("other_problem")].filter(Boolean).join(" \u2013 "), description: v("description")
      });
      if (res.ok) { afterSubmit(res.id); }
      return res;
    };
  }
  var forms = {
    student: initReportForm("st", { dataByGrade: studentData, onSubmit: submitReport("student") }),
    teacher: initReportForm("tc", { data: teacherData, onSubmit: submitReport("teacher") })
  };
  var formList = [forms.student, forms.teacher];

  // ----- Login gate: only a logged-in student or teacher can report, and they get their own form directly -----
  var cards = { student: document.getElementById("report-card-student"), teacher: document.getElementById("report-card-teacher") };
  var locked = document.getElementById("report-locked"), done = document.getElementById("report-done"), current = null;
  function whoami() { return window.FIXIT_API && window.FIXIT_API.me ? window.FIXIT_API.me() : null; }
  function canReport(u) { return !!u && (u.role === "student" || u.role === "teacher"); }
  function setStep(n) {
    document.querySelectorAll("#report .rsteps li").forEach(function (li) {
      var s = +li.dataset.step; li.classList.toggle("on", s === n); li.classList.toggle("done", s < n);
    });
  }
  function showForm(role, keepScroll) {
    current = role;
    Object.keys(cards).forEach(function (k) { cards[k].hidden = k !== role; });
    if (role) { forms[role].fill(whoami()); }
    if (!keepScroll) { window.scrollTo(0, 0); }
  }
  function applyGate() {
    var u = whoami(), ok = canReport(u);
    locked.hidden = ok;
    if (!ok) {
      var admin = u && u.role === "admin";
      document.getElementById("report-locked-title").textContent = admin ? "Reports are for students and teachers" : "Not logged in yet";
      document.getElementById("report-locked-text").textContent = admin
        ? "You are logged in as Admin. Log in with a student or teacher account to send a report."
        : "Log in first to send a report. Only logged-in students and teachers can report an issue.";
      if (current) { formList.forEach(function (f) { f.reset(); }); }
      banner.hidden = true; done.hidden = true; setStep(1); showForm(null, true); return;
    }
    if (current !== u.role) { showForm(u.role, true); }
    updateSteps();
  }
  // after a successful submit: clear the form, the Services search and the picked issue, ready for the next report
  function afterSubmit(id) {
    formList.forEach(function (f) { f.reset(); });
    document.dispatchEvent(new Event("fixit:clear-search"));
    syncIssue();
    var u = whoami(); showForm(u ? u.role : null);
    done.textContent = "Report submitted! Your report ID is " + id + ". Follow its progress in the Activity tab.";
    done.hidden = false; updateSteps();
    toast("Report submitted");
    if (window.FIXIT_API && window.FIXIT_API.refresh) { window.FIXIT_API.refresh(); }
  }
  // step 2 "Fill in details" is only checked once the required fields are filled; step 3 lights up when the form is ready to send
  function updateSteps() {
    var u = whoami(); if (!canReport(u)) { setStep(1); return; }
    var f = current && cards[current] ? cards[current].querySelector("form") : null;
    setStep(f && f.checkValidity() ? 3 : 2);
  }
  var rep = document.getElementById("report");
  rep.addEventListener("input", function () { if (!done.hidden) { done.hidden = true; } });
  ["input", "change", "click"].forEach(function (ev) { rep.addEventListener(ev, function () { setTimeout(updateSteps, 0); }); });

  // ----- From Services: "Report this" lands here with the issue already chosen -----
  var banner = document.getElementById("report-picked");

  function syncIssue() {
    var sel = window.FIXIT_SELECTION;
    if (!sel) {
      banner.hidden = true;
      formList.forEach(function (f) { f.clearIssue(); });
      return;
    }
    formList.forEach(function (f) { f.applyIssue(sel); });
    document.getElementById("rpTxt").textContent = sel.category + " \u203A " + sel.issue;
    document.getElementById("rpSub").textContent = sel.service + " \u00B7 " + sel.group;
    banner.hidden = false;
  }

  document.addEventListener("fixit:select", syncIssue);                       // a new option was tapped
  document.querySelector('nav button[data-v="report"]')
    .addEventListener("click", syncIssue);                                    // "Report this" (and the Report tab)
  document.getElementById("rpChange").addEventListener("click", function () {
    document.querySelector('nav button[data-v="services"]').click();          // back to Services to pick another
  });
  syncIssue();
  document.querySelector('nav button[data-v="report"]').addEventListener("click", applyGate);
  document.addEventListener("fixit:auth", applyGate);                          // login / logout
  document.addEventListener("fixit:profile", function () { var u = whoami(); if (u && current === u.role && forms[u.role]) { forms[u.role].fill(u); } }); // my profile was edited: show the new details
  applyGate();
})();

/* FIXIT report tracking + roles.
   DEMO NOTE: this site has no server, so the "API" below (the `api` object) stores data in the browser.
   Browser storage can be edited by anyone with dev tools, so this is a demo of the rules, not real security.

   Students do not log in at all: they submit a report with their own name, their section, and their
   adviser's name typed in (not checked against anything). The report is then visible only to whichever
   teacher is currently logged in as the adviser of that same section -- matched by SECTION, not by account.
   Teachers must log in. There are two kinds of teacher login:
     - "has an advisory class": picks a section, enters that section's password ("<Section>2026"), then
       types their name. They can then approve/reject reports from students in that section.
     - "does not have an advisory class": just types their name and goes straight to the home page. They
       have no approval queue, but can still file their own reports like any teacher.
   Admin is unchanged from before. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var $ = function (s, r) { return (r || document).querySelector(s); }, $$ = function (s, r) { return [].slice.call((r || document).querySelectorAll(s)); };
  var esc = function (s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]; }); };
  var fmt = function (t) { return new Date(t).toLocaleString('en-PH', { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' }); };
  var mem = {}, memOnly = {};
  function ld(store, k, d) { if (memOnly[k] && mem[k] !== undefined) return mem[k]; try { var v = store.getItem(k); return v ? JSON.parse(v) : d; } catch (e) { return mem[k] !== undefined ? mem[k] : d; } }
  function sv(store, k, v) { mem[k] = v; try { store.setItem(k, JSON.stringify(v)); delete memOnly[k]; } catch (e) { memOnly[k] = 1; } }
  /* Reports: with the server, every browser tab keeps its OWN copy (sessionStorage = private to the tab). Different roles open in
     different tabs of one browser used to overwrite each other's list in localStorage, which made the page flicker, empty out,
     jump to the top and show wrong numbers. Without a server (demo) the tabs still share localStorage on purpose. */
  function repStore() { return (SYNC.on || SYNC.token) ? sessionStorage : localStorage; }
  var L = function (k, d) { return ld(k === 'fixit.reports' ? repStore() : localStorage, k, d); }, S = function (k, v) { sv(k === 'fixit.reports' ? repStore() : localStorage, k, v); if (k === 'fixit.reports') pushReports(); };
  var toast = function (m) { var t = $('#toast'); if (!t) return; t.textContent = m; t.classList.add('on'); setTimeout(function () { t.classList.remove('on'); }, 3200); };

  /* ---------- the two grade levels' sections (also used as the teacher advisory login list) ---------- */
  var SECTIONS = {
    '12': ['Descartes', 'Euler', 'Einstein', 'Euclid', 'Fibonacci', 'Laplace', 'Leibniz', 'Newton', 'Pascal', 'Pearson',
      'Philip Crosby', 'Elton Mayo', 'Douglas McGregor', 'Luca Pacioli', 'Aquinas', 'Aurelius', 'Confucius', 'Freud', 'Mencius', 'Shakespeare'],
    '11': ['Luminaries', 'Pathfinders', 'Practitioners', 'Thinkers', 'Innovators', 'Developers', 'Designers', 'Diplomats', 'Empiricists',
      'Strategists', 'Voyagers', 'Advocates', 'Ambassadors', 'Analysts', 'Aspirers', 'Builders', 'Catalysts', 'Conquerors']
  };

  /* ---------- wording (exactly as specified) ---------- */
  var STAGES = [
    { n: 1, t: 'Report an Issue', d: 'The issue has been sent to the adviser and the admin.' },
    { n: 2, t: 'Waiting for the Approval of the Advisers', d: 'The report has been submitted to the advisers and is waiting for their approval.' },
    { n: 3, t: 'In Validation for the Approval of the Admins and Principal', d: 'The report has been approved by the advisers and is now in the administration office for the approval of the admin and the principal.' },
    { n: 4, t: 'Report Accepted/Reviewed', d: 'The report has been reviewed and is in transit for utilization.' },
    { n: 5, t: 'Report in Action', d: 'Action has been taken on the report, and the issue is in the process of being repaired or resolved.' },
    { n: 6, t: 'Report Accomplished', d: 'The report has been accomplished, and the issue is ready to use and has been reviewed.' }
  ];
  var MSG_DELAY = 'We, the administrators, are deeply apologizing for the delay of the report. This might be due to insufficient funds, bad weather, and lack of tools for the wanted report.';
  var MSG_DONE = 'We, the administrators, are thankful for this report. We immediately took action to resolve this issue. Thank you for your time.';

  /* ---------- TEMPORARY admin test account (development only). Password stored only as a salted PBKDF2 hash.
     To use a real account later: replace this entry (or the `users` store) and delete temp:true. Teachers and
     students no longer have stored accounts -- see the login flow below. ---------- */
  var SEED = [
    { id: 'u-admin', username: 'Admin', name: 'Admin', role: 'admin', salt: '7158a89d9e90540edce0f615644edd7b', hash: 'a955bb28fa801013fd9ab0b79b1b50be7b98353755f1e1d25e28a31c2a52fe78' }
  ];
  /* always refresh the built-in admin account so old saved test accounts (admin.demo) are replaced */
  (function () { var cur = L('fixit.users', []).filter(function (u) { return u.id !== 'u-admin'; }); S('fixit.users', SEED.concat(cur)); })();

  /* ---------- data helpers ---------- */
  var users = function () { return L('fixit.users', []); }, reports = function () { return L('fixit.reports', []); };
  var students = function () { return L('fixit.students', []); };
  var stById = function (id) { return students().filter(function (u) { return u.id === id; })[0]; };
  var uById = function (id) { return users().filter(function (u) { return u.id === id; })[0]; };

  /* A logged-in person is the admin account, a student account, or a teacher account (teachers create their own). me() returns one shape for both so the rest of the code doesn't care. */
  function me() {
    var s = ld(sessionStorage, 'fixit.session', null);
    if (!s) return null;
    if (s.kind === 'admin') { var u = uById(s.uid); return u ? { role: 'admin', id: u.id, name: ld(localStorage, 'fixit.adminname', null) || u.name, temp: u.temp } : null; }
    if (s.kind === 'student') { var st = stById(s.uid); return st ? { role: 'student', id: st.id, name: st.name, lrn: st.lrn, grade: st.grade, section: st.section, adviser: st.adviser } : null; }
    if (s.kind === 'teacher') return { role: 'teacher', id: s.id || ('teacher:' + String(s.name).trim().toLowerCase()), name: s.name, advisory: !!s.advisory, section: s.section || null };
    return null;
  }

  var err = function (m, kind) { return { ok: false, error: m, kind: kind }; };

  function canView(u, r) {
    if (!u) return false;
    if (u.role === 'student') return r.reporterRole === 'student' && r.reporterId === u.id; /* only your own reports */
    if (u.role === 'admin') return r.approval === 'approved' || r.approval === 'none' || (r.approval === 'rejected' && r.rejectedBy === 'admin'); /* never before adviser approval */
    if (u.role === 'teacher') {
      if (u.advisory && r.reporterRole === 'student' && r.section === u.section) return true; /* the section you advise */
      if (r.reporterRole === 'teacher' && r.reporterId === u.id) return true; /* your own reports */
      return false;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function allowedStages(r) { if (r.approval === 'rejected') return []; return r.reporterRole === 'teacher' ? [3, 4, 5, 6] : (r.approval === 'approved' ? [3, 4, 5, 6] : []); }
  var viewT = false;                                                  /* true while a teacher is looking at the page */
  function skip2(r) { return viewT || r.reporterRole === 'teacher'; }   /* teachers never see step 2 "Waiting for the Approval of the Advisers" */
  function dn(r, n) { return skip2(r) ? (n >= 3 ? n - 1 : 1) : n; }          /* teacher reports skip the adviser step, so their steps are numbered 1-5 */
  function totalStages(r) { return skip2(r) ? 5 : 6; }
  function awaitingAdmin(r) { return r.stage === 3 && (r.approval === 'approved' || (r.reporterRole === 'teacher' && r.approval === 'none')); }
  function log(r, u, action, detail) { r.history.push({ at: Date.now(), by: u.name, role: u.role, action: action, detail: detail || '', stage: r.stage }); r.updatedAt = Date.now(); r.updatedBy = u.name; }
  function mutate(id, fn) {
    var all = reports(), r = all.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0], u = me();
    if (!u) return err('Please log in first.');
    if (!r || !canView(u, r)) return err('Report not found.');
    var res = fn(r, u); if (res && res.ok === false) return res;
    S('fixit.reports', all); return { ok: true };
  }
  function pbkdf2(pw, saltHex) {
    if (!(window.crypto && crypto.subtle)) return Promise.reject(new Error('Secure context required'));
    var salt = new Uint8Array(saltHex.match(/../g).map(function (h) { return parseInt(h, 16); }));
    return crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', new TextEncoder().encode(pw), 'PBKDF2', false, ['deriveBits'])
      .then(function (k) { return crypto.subtle.deriveBits({ name: 'PBKDF2', salt: salt, iterations: 60000, hash: 'SHA-256' }, k, 256); })
      .then(function (b) { return [].map.call(new Uint8Array(b), function (x) { return ('0' + x.toString(16)).slice(-2); }).join(''); });
  }

  function initials(n) { var p = String(n || '?').replace(/,/g, ' ').trim().split(/\s+/); return ((p[0] || '?')[0] + ((p[1] || '')[0] || '')).toUpperCase(); }
  /* every report gets the next number: RP-01, RP-02 ... RP-26 (the number is never reused) */
  function nextId() {
    var n = +L('fixit.rpseq', 0) || 0;
    reports().forEach(function (r) { var m = /^RP-(\d+)$/.exec(r.id); if (m && +m[1] > n) n = +m[1]; });
    n++; S('fixit.rpseq', n); return 'RP-' + (n < 10 ? '0' + n : n);
  }
  function lockedHTML() {
    return '<div class="locked card"><span class="locked__ic"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="5" y="11" width="14" height="9" rx="2"/><path d="M8 11V8a4 4 0 0 1 8 0v3"/></svg></span>' +
      '<b>Not logged in yet</b><p>Log in to your account to see your reports and follow their progress.</p></div>';
  }
  function studentInfo(u) { return u.role === 'student' ? '<p class="note">LRN ' + esc(u.lrn) + ' &middot; Grade ' + esc(u.grade) + ' &middot; ' + esc(u.section) + '<br>Adviser: ' + esc(u.adviser) + '</p>' : ''; }

  /* An adviser counts as "logged in" while their browser keeps checking in (every 30 s, valid for 90 s).
     DEMO NOTE: this lives in browser storage, so it only works across tabs of one browser. A real server would track it. */
  function presence() { return L('fixit.presence', {}); }
  function setPresence(section) { var p = presence(); p[section] = { at: Date.now() }; S('fixit.presence', p); SYNC && SYNC.presence(true); }
  function clearPresence(section) { var p = presence(); delete p[section]; S('fixit.presence', p); SYNC && SYNC.presence(false); }
  function adviserOnline(section) { var e = presence()[section]; return !!e && Date.now() - e.at < 90000; }
  function beat() { var u = me(); if (u && u.role === 'teacher' && u.advisory) setPresence(u.section); }
  setInterval(beat, 30000); beat();

/* ---------- SHARED SERVER (used when the site is opened from the FIXIT server; otherwise everything stays in this browser) ---------- */
  var SYNC = { on: false, token: ld(sessionStorage, 'fixit.token', null), dirty: 0, busy: false, busySince: 0, again: false, rev: null, gen: 0, awaiting: null };
  var PHOTO = { url: {}, pending: {}, loading: {}, fail: {} }; /* report photos: shown pictures, new ones still being sent, ones being fetched */
  /* every request has a time limit, so one stuck request can never freeze updates (this was the cause of "log in again to see changes") */
  SYNC.fetch = function (url, opts, ms) {
    opts = opts || {};
    if (window.AbortController) { var ac = new AbortController(); opts.signal = ac.signal; setTimeout(function () { ac.abort(); }, ms || 30000); }
    return fetch(url, opts);
  };
  SYNC.setToken = function (t) { SYNC.token = t; sv(sessionStorage, 'fixit.token', t); };
  SYNC.hdr = function () { var h = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }; if (SYNC.token) h.Authorization = 'Bearer ' + SYNC.token; return h; };
  SYNC.post = function (url, obj, method) {
    return SYNC.fetch(url, { method: method || 'POST', headers: SYNC.hdr(), body: JSON.stringify(obj) }, 30000).then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
      .catch(function () { return { ok: false, error: 'Cannot reach the server. Check your internet connection and try again.' }; });
  };
  SYNC.presence = function (on) { if (SYNC.on && SYNC.token) SYNC.post('/api/presence', { on: !!on }); };
  SYNC.logout = function () {
    if (!SYNC.on) return;
    var cu = me(); if (cu && cu.advisory) SYNC.presence(false);
    SYNC.setToken(null); SYNC.awaiting = null; sv(repStore(), 'fixit.reports', []);
  };
  SYNC.content = function (c) {
    if (!SYNC.on || !SYNC.token) return;
    var imgs = function (x) { var n = 0; ['grand', 'latest', 'events'].forEach(function (t) { ((x && x[t]) || []).forEach(function (i) { if (i.img) n++; }); }); return n; };
    var mineImgs = imgs(c);
    SYNC.gen++; /* tells any request already on its way that its answer may be older than this save */
    SYNC.post('/api/content', { content: c }, 'PUT').then(function (j) {
      SYNC.gen++;
      if (!j.ok) { toast(j.error || 'Could not save to the server.'); SYNC.pull(true); return; }
      if (j.rev !== undefined) SYNC.rev = j.rev;
      if (j.content && JSON.stringify(j.content) !== JSON.stringify(window.FIXIT_HOME.get())) { /* show exactly what everyone else will see */
        sv(localStorage, 'fixit.content', j.content); window.FIXIT_HOME.render();
        var cu1 = me(); if (cu1 && cu1.role === 'admin' && $('#c-list')) contentList();
        if (imgs(j.content) < mineImgs) toast('A photo was too large to share. Please use a smaller photo.');
      }
    });
  };
  function applyContent(st) {
    if (!st) return;
    if (st.content !== undefined) { /* the server only sends content when it changed */
      if (st.content && JSON.stringify(st.content) !== JSON.stringify(window.FIXIT_HOME.get())) {
        sv(localStorage, 'fixit.content', st.content); window.FIXIT_HOME.render();
        var cu0 = me(); if (cu0 && cu0.role === 'admin' && $('#c-list')) contentList();
      } else if (st.content === null) { /* the server has no homepage content (it was cleared): drop the old copy kept in this browser too */
        try { localStorage.removeItem('fixit.content'); } catch (e) { }
        window.FIXIT_HOME.render();
        var cu00 = me(); if (cu00 && cu00.role === 'admin' && $('#c-list')) contentList();
      }
      if (st.contentRev !== undefined) SYNC.rev = st.contentRev;
    }
    if (st.presence) { var p = {}; Object.keys(st.presence).forEach(function (k) { p[k] = { at: Date.now() - st.presence[k] }; }); sv(localStorage, 'fixit.presence', p); }
  }
  function applyProfile(p) { /* copy my saved profile from the server into this browser; returns true when something changed */
    if (!p || typeof p !== 'object') return false;
    var ss = ld(sessionStorage, 'fixit.session', null); if (!ss) return false;
    if (p.role === 'student' && ss.kind === 'student' && ss.uid === p.id) {
      var cur = stById(p.id) || {}, nx = { id: p.id, lrn: p.lrn, name: p.name, grade: p.grade, section: p.section, adviser: p.adviser };
      if (['name', 'grade', 'section', 'adviser', 'lrn'].some(function (k) { return cur[k] !== nx[k]; })) {
        sv(localStorage, 'fixit.students', students().filter(function (x) { return x.id !== p.id; }).concat([Object.assign({}, cur, nx)])); return true;
      }
    } else if (p.role === 'teacher' && ss.kind === 'teacher' && ss.id === p.id) {
      if (ss.name !== p.name || !!ss.advisory !== !!p.advisory || (ss.section || null) !== (p.section || null)) {
        sv(sessionStorage, 'fixit.session', { kind: 'teacher', id: p.id, advisory: !!p.advisory, section: p.section || null, name: p.name }); return true;
      }
    } else if (p.role === 'admin' && ss.kind === 'admin') {
      var an = ld(localStorage, 'fixit.adminname', null), want = p.custom ? p.name : null;
      if (an !== want) { sv(localStorage, 'fixit.adminname', want); return true; }
    }
    return false;
  }
  function applyState(st) {
    if (!st || !st.ok) return;
    applyContent(st);
    if (SYNC.token && st.auth === false) { /* login ran out (12 h) or the server was reset */
      SYNC.setToken(null); sv(sessionStorage, 'fixit.session', null); sv(repStore(), 'fixit.reports', []);
      renderAuth(); renderAct(true); toast('Please log in again.'); return;
    }
    if (st.profile && applyProfile(st.profile)) { renderAuth(); document.dispatchEvent(new Event('fixit:profile')); } /* my details were changed (here or on another device) */
    var mine = Array.isArray(st.reports) ? st.reports : [], awChanged = false;
    if (typeof st.awaiting === 'number' && st.awaiting !== SYNC.awaiting) { SYNC.awaiting = st.awaiting; awChanged = true; }
    if (JSON.stringify(mine) !== JSON.stringify(L('fixit.reports', []))) { sv(repStore(), 'fixit.reports', mine); renderAct(false); }
    else if (awChanged) renderAct(false);
  }
  SYNC.pull = function (force) {
    if (!SYNC.on) return Promise.resolve();
    if (SYNC.busy && Date.now() - SYNC.busySince > 12000) SYNC.busy = false; /* a stuck request must never stop updates */
    if (!force && SYNC.busy) return Promise.resolve();
    SYNC.busy = true; SYNC.busySince = Date.now();
    var gen = SYNC.gen, url = '/api/state' + (SYNC.rev !== null ? '?rev=' + SYNC.rev : '');
    return SYNC.fetch(url, { headers: SYNC.hdr(), cache: 'no-store' }, 8000).then(function (r) { return r.json(); }).then(function (j) {
      if (!j || !j.ok) return;
      if (gen !== SYNC.gen) j.content = undefined; /* an admin save happened meanwhile: ignore this possibly older content */
      if (SYNC.dirty) applyContent(j); /* reports are being sent: still show new homepage content right away */
      else applyState(j);
    }).catch(function () {}).then(function () { SYNC.busy = false; });
  };
  SYNC.pullNow = function () { SYNC.dirty = 0; return SYNC.pull(true); };
  var pushTimer = null, pushing = false;
  function pushReports() {
    if (!SYNC.on || !SYNC.token) return;
    SYNC.dirty++; clearTimeout(pushTimer); pushTimer = setTimeout(flushReports, 150);
  }
  function flushReports() {
    if (pushing) { SYNC.again = true; return; }
    pushing = true; var sent = SYNC.dirty;
    var out = L('fixit.reports', []).map(function (r) { var ph = PHOTO.pending[r.id]; if (!ph) return r; var c = {}; Object.keys(r).forEach(function (k) { c[k] = r[k]; }); c.photo = ph; return c; });
    SYNC.post('/api/reports', { reports: out }, 'PUT').then(function (j) {
      pushing = false;
      if (j.ok) {
        Object.keys(PHOTO.pending).forEach(function (id) { delete PHOTO.pending[id]; }); /* the server has answered: new photos are saved there now */
        if (SYNC.dirty === sent && !SYNC.again) { SYNC.dirty = 0; applyState({ ok: true, reports: j.reports, awaiting: j.awaiting, content: null, presence: null, auth: true }); }
        else { SYNC.again = false; flushReports(); }
      } else if (/log in/i.test(j.error || '')) { SYNC.dirty = 0; SYNC.pull(true); }
      else { setTimeout(flushReports, 5000); } /* offline: try again soon, nothing is lost */
    });
  }
  function signupDone(j) { if (!j.ok) return err(j.error || 'Something went wrong.'); return { ok: true, created: true }; } /* sign-up only CREATES the account; the person then logs in */
  function finishStudent(j) {
    if (!j.ok) return err(j.error || 'Something went wrong.');
    SYNC.setToken(j.token); var st = j.student;
    sv(localStorage, 'fixit.students', students().filter(function (x) { return x.id !== st.id; }).concat([st]));
    sv(sessionStorage, 'fixit.session', { kind: 'student', uid: st.id });
    return SYNC.pullNow().then(function () { return { ok: true }; });
  }
  function finishTeacher(j) {
    if (!j.ok) return err(j.error || 'Something went wrong.');
    SYNC.setToken(j.token); var t = j.teacher;
    sv(sessionStorage, 'fixit.session', { kind: 'teacher', id: t.id, advisory: !!t.advisory, section: t.section || null, name: t.name });
    return SYNC.pullNow().then(function () { return { ok: true, advisory: !!t.advisory }; });
  }
  SYNC.start = function () {
    if (location.protocol === 'file:') return;
    fetch('/api/state', { cache: 'no-store' }).then(function (r) { return r.json(); }).then(function (j) {
      if (!j || !j.ok || j.serverTime === undefined) return;
      SYNC.on = true; window.FIXIT_SYNC = { content: SYNC.content, isOn: function () { return SYNC.on; }, portalFile: function (o) { return SYNC.post('/api/admin/portal-file', o); }, portalDelete: function (o) { return SYNC.post('/api/admin/portal-file-delete', o); } };
      try { localStorage.removeItem('fixit.reports'); } catch (e) { } /* drop the old shared copy left by earlier versions */
      setInterval(function () { SYNC.pull(false); }, 3000);
      document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { if (!document.hidden) SYNC.pull(false); });
      window.addEventListener('online', function () { SYNC.pull(true); });
      window.addEventListener('focus', function () { SYNC.pull(false); });
      if (!SYNC.token) { sv(repStore(), 'fixit.reports', []); }
      SYNC.pull(true);
    }).catch(function () {});
  };
  window.FIXIT_SYNC = window.FIXIT_SYNC || { content: function () {} };

    /* ---------- the "API": every rule is enforced here, never trusting the interface ---------- */
  var api = {
    loginAdmin: function (name, pw) {
      if (SYNC.on) return SYNC.post('/api/login/admin', { username: name, password: pw }).then(function (j) {
        if (!j.ok) return err(j.error || 'Wrong username or password.');
        SYNC.setToken(j.token); sv(sessionStorage, 'fixit.session', { kind: 'admin', uid: 'u-admin' });
        return SYNC.pullNow().then(function () { return { ok: true }; });
      });
      var u = users().filter(function (x) { return String(x.username).toLowerCase() === String(name).trim().toLowerCase(); })[0];
      if (!u) return pbkdf2(pw, '00').then(function () { return err('Wrong username or password.'); }, function () { return err('Wrong username or password.'); });
      return pbkdf2(pw, u.salt).then(function (h) {
        if (h !== u.hash) return err('Wrong username or password.');
        sv(sessionStorage, 'fixit.session', { kind: 'admin', uid: u.id }); return { ok: true };
      }, function () { return err('Login needs a secure page (https or localhost).'); });
    },
    /* Teachers have real accounts now (username + password), created by the teachers themselves. This needs the FIXIT server. */
    loginTeacher: function (username, pw) {
      if (!SYNC.on) return err('Open the website from the FIXIT server (http://localhost:3000) to use teacher accounts.');
      if (!String(username || '').trim() || !pw) return err('Please enter your username and password.');
      return SYNC.post('/api/login/teacher', { username: String(username).trim(), password: pw }).then(finishTeacher);
    },
    registerTeacher: function (p) {
      if (!SYNC.on) return err('Open the website from the FIXIT server (http://localhost:3000) to create a teacher account.');
      if (p.type !== 'advising' && p.type !== 'none') return err('Please choose if you have an advising class or not.');
      if (!String(p.name || '').trim()) return err('Please enter your name.');
      if (p.type === 'advising' && !p.section) return err('Please choose the section you advise.');
      if (!String(p.username || '').trim()) return err('Please choose a username.');
      if (String(p.password || '').length < 8) return err('Password needs at least 8 characters.');
      if (p.password !== p.password2) return err('Passwords do not match.');
      if (!String(p.code || '').trim()) return err('Please enter the teacher code from the admin.');
      return SYNC.post('/api/register/teacher', { type: p.type, section: p.section, name: String(p.name).trim(), username: String(p.username).trim(), password: p.password, code: String(p.code).trim() }).then(signupDone);
    },
    logout: function () { var cu = me(); SYNC.logout(); if (cu && cu.advisory && !SYNC.on) clearPresence(cu.section); sv(sessionStorage, 'fixit.session', null); },
    /* Edit MY OWN profile. The account always comes from the login (never from the request), so nobody can change anybody else's account;
       the server checks every rule again. */
    updateProfile: function (p) {
      var u = me(); if (!u) return Promise.resolve(err('Please log in again.'));
      var name = String(p.name || '').trim(), pw = String(p.newPassword || '');
      if (!name) return Promise.resolve(err('Please enter your name.'));
      if (pw) {
        if (!p.currentPassword) return Promise.resolve(err('Please enter your current password to set a new one.'));
        if (pw !== p.newPassword2) return Promise.resolve(err('The two new passwords do not match.'));
        var minPw = u.role === 'student' ? 6 : 8;
        if (pw.length < minPw) return Promise.resolve(err('Your new password needs at least ' + minPw + ' characters.'));
      }
      if (u.role === 'student') {
        if (!p.grade || !p.section || (SECTIONS[p.grade] || []).indexOf(p.section) < 0) return Promise.resolve(err('Please choose your grade level and section.'));
        if (!String(p.adviser || '').trim()) return Promise.resolve(err("Please enter your adviser's name."));
      }
      if (u.role === 'teacher') {
        if (p.type !== 'advising' && p.type !== 'none') return Promise.resolve(err('Please choose if you have an advising class or not.'));
        if (p.type === 'advising' && !p.section) return Promise.resolve(err('Please choose the section you advise.'));
      }
      if (!SYNC.on && SYNC.token && location.protocol !== 'file:') return Promise.resolve(err('Still connecting to the server. Please try again in a moment.'));
      if (SYNC.on) {
        var body = { name: name };
        if (pw) { body.currentPassword = p.currentPassword; body.newPassword = pw; }
        if (u.role === 'student') { body.grade = p.grade; body.section = p.section; body.adviser = String(p.adviser).trim(); }
        if (u.role === 'teacher') { body.type = p.type; body.section = p.type === 'advising' ? p.section : null; }
        return SYNC.post('/api/account', body, 'PUT').then(function (j) {
          if (!j || !j.ok) return err((j && j.error) || 'Something went wrong.');
          if (j.token) SYNC.setToken(j.token);
          applyProfile(j.profile);
          SYNC.pull(true); /* refresh the reports this account may see (a new section can change them) */
          return { ok: true };
        });
      }
      /* no server (demo mode): the profile is kept in this browser */
      if (u.role === 'admin') {
        if (pw) return Promise.resolve(err('Open the website from the FIXIT server (http://localhost:3000) to change the password.'));
        sv(localStorage, 'fixit.adminname', name); return Promise.resolve({ ok: true });
      }
      if (u.role === 'student') {
        var recs = students(), rec = recs.filter(function (x) { return x.id === u.id; })[0];
        if (!rec) return Promise.resolve(err('Account not found.'));
        var done = function (salt, hash) {
          var upd = Object.assign({}, rec, { name: name, grade: p.grade, section: p.section, adviser: String(p.adviser).trim() });
          if (salt) { upd.salt = salt; upd.hash = hash; }
          S('fixit.students', recs.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== u.id; }).concat([upd])); return { ok: true };
        };
        if (!pw) return Promise.resolve(done());
        if (!rec.salt || !rec.hash) return Promise.resolve(err('Open the website from the FIXIT server to change the password.'));
        return pbkdf2(p.currentPassword, rec.salt).then(function (h) {
          if (h !== rec.hash) return err('Your current password is wrong.');
          var salt = [].map.call(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)), function (x) { return ('0' + x.toString(16)).slice(-2); }).join('');
          return pbkdf2(pw, salt).then(function (nh) { return done(salt, nh); });
        }, function () { return err('This needs a secure page (https or localhost).'); });
      }
      return Promise.resolve(err('Open the website from the FIXIT server (http://localhost:3000) to change a teacher account.'));
    },
    submit: function (p) {
      var now = Date.now();
      if (p.role === 'student') {
        var su = me();
        if (!su || su.role !== 'student') return err('Please log in with your student account before sending a report.');
        if (!p.title || !p.description || p.description.length < 5) return err('Please fill in the problem type and description.');
        if (!adviserOnline(su.section)) return err('Unable to process the report. The adviser is not logged in. Please ask the adviser to log in and Report again.');
        var r = {
          id: nextId(), title: p.title, description: p.description, location: p.location, category: p.category,
          reporterId: su.id, reporterName: su.name, reporterRole: 'student', section: su.section, adviserName: su.adviser,
          stage: 2, approval: 'pending', delayed: false, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now, updatedBy: su.name, history: []
        };
        attachPhoto(r, p.photo);
        r.history.push({ at: now, by: r.reporterName, role: 'student', action: 'Report submitted', detail: 'Sent to the ' + su.section + ' adviser and the admin', stage: 1 });
        r.history.push({ at: now, by: 'System', role: 'system', action: 'Waiting for adviser approval', detail: '', stage: 2 });
        var all = reports(); all.push(r); S('fixit.reports', all);
        return { ok: true, id: r.id };
      }
      var u = me();
      if (!u || u.role !== 'teacher') return err('Please log in from the Profile tab as a teacher before submitting.');
      if (!p.title || !p.description || p.description.length < 5) return err('Please fill in the problem type and description.');
      var r2 = {
        id: nextId(), title: p.title, description: p.description, location: p.location, category: p.category,
        reporterId: u.id, reporterName: u.name, reporterRole: 'teacher', section: null, adviserName: null,
        stage: 3, approval: 'none', delayed: false, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now, updatedBy: u.name, history: []
      };
      attachPhoto(r2, p.photo);
      r2.history.push({ at: now, by: u.name, role: 'teacher', action: 'Report submitted', detail: 'Sent directly to the admin', stage: 1 });
      r2.history.push({ at: now, by: 'System', role: 'system', action: 'Waiting for admin validation', detail: '', stage: 3 });
      var all2 = reports(); all2.push(r2); S('fixit.reports', all2);
      return { ok: true, id: r2.id };
    },
    list: function () { var u = me(); return u ? reports().filter(function (r) { return canView(u, r); }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.createdAt - a.createdAt; }) : []; },
    registerStudent: function (p) { /* every student gets their own account (LRN + password) */
      var lrn = String(p.lrn || '').replace(/\s/g, '');
      if (!/^\d{12}$/.test(lrn)) return Promise.resolve(err('Enter your 12-digit LRN (Learner Reference Number).'));
      if (!p.name || !p.name.trim()) return Promise.resolve(err('Please enter your name.'));
      if (!p.grade || !p.section || (SECTIONS[p.grade] || []).indexOf(p.section) < 0) return Promise.resolve(err('Please choose your grade level and section.'));
      if (!p.adviser || !p.adviser.trim()) return Promise.resolve(err("Please enter your adviser's name."));
      if (!p.password || p.password.length < 6) return Promise.resolve(err('Your password needs at least 6 characters.'));
      if (p.password !== p.password2) return Promise.resolve(err('The two passwords do not match.'));
      if (!SYNC.on && students().filter(function (x) { return x.lrn === lrn; })[0]) return Promise.resolve(err('That LRN already has an account. Please log in instead.')); /* with the server, only the SERVER decides (the browser's own copy can be old after accounts were cleared) */
      if (SYNC.on) return SYNC.post('/api/register/student', { lrn: lrn, name: p.name.trim(), grade: p.grade, section: p.section, adviser: p.adviser.trim(), password: p.password }).then(signupDone);
      var salt = [].map.call(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)), function (x) { return ('0' + x.toString(16)).slice(-2); }).join('');
      return pbkdf2(p.password, salt).then(function (h) {
        var st = { id: 's-' + lrn, lrn: lrn, name: p.name.trim(), grade: p.grade, section: p.section, adviser: p.adviser.trim(), salt: salt, hash: h, createdAt: Date.now() };
        S('fixit.students', students().concat([st]));
        return { ok: true, created: true };
      }, function () { return err('Sign up needs a secure page (https or localhost).'); });
    },
    loginStudent: function (lrn, pw) {
      if (SYNC.on) return SYNC.post('/api/login/student', { lrn: String(lrn || '').replace(/\s/g, ''), password: pw || '' }).then(finishStudent);
      var u = students().filter(function (x) { return x.lrn === String(lrn || '').replace(/\s/g, ''); })[0];
      if (!u) return Promise.resolve(err('Wrong LRN or password.'));
      return pbkdf2(pw || '', u.salt).then(function (h) {
        if (h !== u.hash) return err('Wrong LRN or password.');
        sv(sessionStorage, 'fixit.session', { kind: 'student', uid: u.id }); return { ok: true };
      }, function () { return err('Login needs a secure page (https or localhost).'); });
    },
    pendingCount: function () { var u = me(); return u && u.role === 'admin' ? reports().filter(function (r) { return r.approval === 'pending'; }).length : 0; }, /* count only, no details */
    decide: function (id, approve, note) {
      return mutate(id, function (r, u) {
        if (!(u.role === 'teacher' && u.advisory && r.section === u.section)) return err('You are not the adviser logged in for this section.');
        if (r.approval !== 'pending') return err('This report was already reviewed.');
        r.approval = approve ? 'approved' : 'rejected'; r.approvalBy = u.name; r.approvalAt = Date.now(); r.approvalNote = note || '';
        if (approve) r.stage = 3; log(r, u, approve ? 'Approved by adviser' : 'Rejected by adviser', note);
      });
    },
    adminDecide: function (id, approve, note) { /* Stage 3: the admin approves the report or rejects it with a reason */
      return mutate(id, function (r, u) {
        if (u.role !== 'admin') return err('Only the admin can do this.');
        if (!awaitingAdmin(r)) return err('This report is not waiting for admin validation.');
        if (approve) { r.stage = 4; r.delayFixedAt = null; log(r, u, 'Approved by the admin', 'Stage ' + dn(r, 4) + ': ' + STAGES[3].t); }
        else { r.approval = 'rejected'; r.rejectedBy = 'admin'; r.approvalBy = u.name; r.approvalAt = Date.now(); r.approvalNote = note || ''; r.delayed = false; log(r, u, 'Rejected by the admin', note || ''); }
      });
    },
    decideMany: function (sid, approve, note) { /* every waiting report of ONE student, in one go */
      var u = me(); if (!u || u.role !== 'teacher' || !u.advisory) return err('Only the adviser can do this.');
      var ids = reports().filter(function (r) { return r.reporterId === sid && r.section === u.section && r.approval === 'pending'; }).map(function (r) { return r.id; }), done = 0;
      ids.forEach(function (id) { if (api.decide(id, approve, note).ok) done++; });
      return done ? { ok: true, count: done } : err('There are no waiting reports for this student.');
    },
    setStage: function (id, n) {
      return mutate(id, function (r, u) {
        if (u.role !== 'admin') return err('Only the admin can change progress.');
        if (allowedStages(r).indexOf(n) < 0) return err('That stage is not allowed for this report.');
        if (n <= r.stage) return err('Progress can only move forward. It cannot be taken back.');
        if (awaitingAdmin(r)) return err('Approve this report at the validation stage first.');
        r.stage = n; r.delayFixedAt = null; if (n === 6) { r.delayed = false; r.accomplishedAt = Date.now(); } else r.accomplishedAt = null;
        log(r, u, n === 6 ? 'Marked as accomplished' : 'Stage changed', 'Stage ' + dn(r, n) + ': ' + STAGES[n - 1].t);
      });
    },
    setDelay: function (id, on) {
      return mutate(id, function (r, u) {
        if (u.role !== 'admin') return err('Only the admin can mark a delay.');
        if (!allowedStages(r).length || r.stage === 6) return err('This report cannot be marked as delayed.');
        r.delayed = !!on; r.delayFixedAt = on ? null : Date.now();
        log(r, u, on ? 'Marked as delayed' : 'Delay fixed', on ? '' : 'The delay has been fixed and the process is continuing.');
      });
    },
    content: function () { return window.FIXIT_HOME.get(); },
