    contentAdd: function (type, item) {
      var u = me(); if (!u || u.role !== 'admin') return err('Admins only.');
      var c = api.content(); if (!c[type]) return err('Unknown type.');
      item.id = 'c' + Date.now(); c[type].push(item);
      if (!window.FIXIT_HOME.save(c)) { c[type].pop(); return err('Could not save. The photo may be too large or browser storage is full.'); }
      window.FIXIT_HOME.render(); return { ok: true };
    },
    contentEdit: function (type, id, patch) {
      var u = me(); if (!u || u.role !== 'admin') return err('Admins only.');
      var c = api.content(), list = c[type]; if (!list) return err('Unknown type.');
      var it = list.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0]; if (!it) return err('That entry no longer exists.');
      var before = JSON.stringify(it);
      it.title = patch.title; it.text = patch.text; it.date = patch.date;
      if (patch.img === '') delete it.img; else if (patch.img) it.img = patch.img;
      if (!window.FIXIT_HOME.save(c)) { Object.keys(it).forEach(function (k) { delete it[k]; }); Object.assign(it, JSON.parse(before)); return err('Could not save. The photo may be too large or browser storage is full.'); }
      window.FIXIT_HOME.render(); return { ok: true };
    },
    contentDel: function (type, id) {
      var u = me(); if (!u || u.role !== 'admin') return err('Admins only.');
      var c = api.content(); c[type] = (c[type] || []).filter(function (x) { return x.id !== id; });
      window.FIXIT_HOME.save(c); window.FIXIT_HOME.render(); return { ok: true };
    }
  };

  /* ---------- rendering ---------- */
  function trackerHTML(r) {
    var rej = r.approval === 'rejected', rejAt = r.rejectedBy === 'admin' ? 3 : 2, s2 = skip2(r);
    var steps = STAGES.filter(function (s) { return !(s2 && s.n === 2); });
    var eff = (s2 && r.stage === 2) ? 1 : r.stage;                 /* waiting for the adviser = still on step 1 for teachers */
    var rejStep = (s2 && rejAt === 2) ? 1 : rejAt;
    var total = steps.length, cur = dn(r, r.stage);
    var pct = r.stage === 6 ? 100 : Math.round((cur - 1) / (total - 1) * 100);
    var mc = rej ? ' rej' : r.stage === 6 ? ' done' : r.delayed ? ' late' : '';
    var label = rej ? 'Rejected at stage ' + dn(r, rejAt) : r.stage === 6 ? 'Accomplished' : (r.delayed ? 'Delayed \u00B7 ' : '') + 'Stage ' + cur + ' of ' + total;
    var h = '<div class="pmeter' + mc + '"><div class="pmeter__top"><b>' + label + '</b><span>' + pct + '%</span></div><div class="pmeter__bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div></div><ol class="trk" aria-label="Report progress">';
    steps.forEach(function (s) {
      var cls = 'pend', dot = dn(r, s.n);
      if (rej && s.n === rejStep) { cls = 'cur rej'; dot = '&#10005;'; }
      else if (s.n < eff || (s.n === 6 && r.stage === 6)) { cls = 'done'; dot = '&#10003;'; }
      else if (s.n === eff) { cls = 'cur'; }
      var when = r.history.filter(function (x) { return x.stage === s.n; }).slice(-1)[0];
      var showD = cls.indexOf('cur') > -1 || (s.n === 6 && r.stage === 6);
      h += '<li class="' + cls + '"><span class="dot">' + dot + '</span><div class="trk__c"><b>' + esc(s.t) + '</b>' + (when && cls.indexOf('pend') < 0 ? '<small>' + fmt(when.at) + '</small>' : '') + (showD && !rej ? '<p>' + esc(s.d) + '</p>' : '') + '</div></li>';
    });
    return h + '</ol>';
  }
  function historyHTML(r) {
    return '<div class="hist2"><h4>History</h4><ol>' + r.history.slice().reverse().map(function (x) {
      return '<li class="hi hi--' + esc(x.role) + '"><span class="hi__dot"></span><div class="hi__b"><b>' + esc(x.action) + '</b>' + (x.detail ? '<p>' + esc(x.detail) + '</p>' : '') + '<small>' + esc(x.by) + ' <em>' + esc(x.role) + '</em> &middot; ' + fmt(x.at) + '</small></div></li>';
    }).join('') + '</ol></div>';
  }
  function pill(r) {
    if (r.approval === 'rejected') return '<span class="pill bad">Rejected</span>';
    if (r.stage === 6) return '<span class="pill ok">Accomplished</span>';
    if (r.delayed) return '<span class="pill bad">Delayed · Stage ' + dn(r, r.stage) + '</span>';
    if (r.approval === 'pending') return '<span class="pill wait">Waiting for adviser</span>';
    return '<span class="pill">Stage ' + dn(r, r.stage) + ' of ' + totalStages(r) + '</span>';
  }
  var openIds = {};
  function card(r, u) {
    var h = '<details class="card rc" data-id="' + r.id + '"' + (openIds[r.id] ? ' open' : '') + '><summary><div class="ti"><span class="rid">' + r.id + '</span>' + (newSet[r.id] ? '<span class="newtag">NEW</span>' : '') + '<b>' + esc(r.title) + '</b><small>' + fmt(r.createdAt) + (u.role !== 'student' ? ' &middot; ' + esc(r.reporterName) : '') + '</small></div>' + pill(r) + '</summary><div class="bd">';
    h += infoHTML(r);
    h += trackerHTML(r);
    if (r.approval === 'rejected') h += '<div class="msg rej"><b>REPORT REJECTED</b>Rejected by ' + esc(r.approvalBy) + ' on ' + fmt(r.approvalAt) + '.' + (r.approvalNote ? ' Reason: ' + esc(r.approvalNote) : '') + '</div>';
    if (r.delayed && r.stage !== 6) h += '<div class="msg delay"><b>REPORT DELAYED</b>' + esc(MSG_DELAY) + '</div>';
    if (!r.delayed && r.delayFixedAt && r.stage !== 6 && r.approval !== 'rejected') h += '<div class="msg fixed"><b>DELAY FIXED</b>Good news: the delay on this report has been fixed and the process is continuing. Thank you for your patience.</div>';
    if (r.stage === 6 && r.approval !== 'rejected') h += '<div class="msg done"><b>REPORT ACCOMPLISHED</b>' + esc(MSG_DONE) + '</div>';
    if (u.role === 'teacher' && u.advisory && r.section === u.section && r.approval === 'pending')
      h += reviewHTML(r);
    if (u.role === 'admin' && awaitingAdmin(r)) h += adminReviewHTML(r);
    if (u.role === 'admin' && r.approval !== 'rejected' && r.stage !== 6) {
      var fwd = allowedStages(r).filter(function (n) { return n > r.stage && n !== 6; });
      if (!awaitingAdmin(r) && fwd.length) h += '<label>Move progress forward (Admin)</label><p class="note">Progress only moves forward and cannot be taken back.</p><div class="stagebar">' + fwd.map(function (n) { return '<button class="btn alt" data-act="stage" data-id="' + r.id + '" data-n="' + n + '">' + dn(r, n) + '. ' + esc(STAGES[n - 1].t.split(' ').slice(0, 3).join(' ')) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
      h += '<button class="btn warn" data-act="delay" data-id="' + r.id + '" data-on="' + (r.delayed ? 0 : 1) + '">' + (r.delayed ? 'Remove delayed flag' : 'Mark as delayed') + '</button>' + (awaitingAdmin(r) ? '' : '<button class="btn good" data-act="stage" data-id="' + r.id + '" data-n="6">Mark as accomplished</button>');
    }
    if (u.role !== 'student') h += historyHTML(r);
    return h + '</div></details>';
  }
  var rejectOpen = {}, rjText = {};
  var IC = {
    photo: '<rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="14" rx="2"/><circle cx="9" cy="11" r="2"/><path d="M21 16l-5-5-8 8"/>',
    tool: '<path d="M14.7 6.3a4 4 0 0 0-5.4 5.4L3 18l3 3 6.3-6.3a4 4 0 0 0 5.4-5.4l-2.6 2.6-2.4-.6-.6-2.4z"/>',
    warn: '<path d="M12 3l10 18H2z"/><path d="M12 10v5M12 18v.5"/>',
    pin: '<path d="M12 21s7-6.2 7-11a7 7 0 1 0-14 0c0 4.8 7 11 7 11z"/><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="2.5"/>',
    doc: '<path d="M6 3h9l4 4v14H6z"/><path d="M9 12h7M9 16h5"/>',
    user: '<circle cx="12" cy="8" r="4"/><path d="M4 21c1-4 4-6 8-6s7 2 8 6"/>',
    clock: '<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 7v5l3 2"/>'
  };
  function irow(label, val, icon) { return '<div class="irow"><span class="irow__ic"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">' + icon + '</svg></span><div class="irow__t"><small>' + label + '</small><div>' + val + '</div></div></div>'; }
  /* report photo: shown from memory when we have it; otherwise fetched from the server the first time the card is opened */
  function photoSrc(r) { return r.photo || PHOTO.url[r.id] || ''; }
  function photoHTML(r) {
    var src = photoSrc(r);
    if (src) return '<img class="rphoto" data-act="photo-zoom" src="' + src + '" alt="Photo of the reported problem">';
    if (PHOTO.fail[r.id]) return '<span class="note">The photo could not be loaded.</span>';
    if (openIds[r.id]) loadPhoto(r.id);
    return '<span class="note">Loading photo...</span>';
  }
  function loadPhoto(id) {
    if (PHOTO.url[id] || PHOTO.loading[id] || !SYNC.on || !SYNC.token) return;
    PHOTO.loading[id] = 1;
    SYNC.fetch('/api/photo/' + encodeURIComponent(id), { headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + SYNC.token }, cache: 'no-store' }, 30000)
      .then(function (res) { if (!res.ok) throw new Error('no photo'); return res.blob(); })
      .then(function (b) { PHOTO.url[id] = URL.createObjectURL(b); delete PHOTO.fail[id]; })
      .catch(function () { PHOTO.fail[id] = 1; })
      .then(function () { delete PHOTO.loading[id]; $$('[data-photo="' + id + '"]').forEach(function (el) { el.innerHTML = photoHTML({ id: id }); }); });
  }
  function attachPhoto(r, photo) {
    if (typeof photo !== 'string' || photo.length > 600000 || !/^data:image\/jpeg;base64,[A-Za-z0-9+\/=]+$/.test(photo)) return;
    r.hasPhoto = true;
    if (SYNC.on) { PHOTO.pending[r.id] = photo; PHOTO.url[r.id] = photo; } else r.photo = photo;
  }
  /* the report details as clear labelled rows */
  function infoHTML(r) {
    var cats = String(r.category || '').split(' \u203A ').filter(Boolean);
    var crumb = cats.map(function (c) { return '<span class="crumb">' + esc(c) + '</span>'; }).join('<i class="crumb__sep">&rsaquo;</i>');
    var sub = [r.section ? 'Section: ' + esc(r.section) : '', r.adviserName ? 'Adviser: ' + esc(r.adviserName) : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' &middot; ');
    return '<div class="rinfo">' + (crumb ? irow('Facility concern', crumb, IC.tool) : '') + irow('Problem', esc(r.title), IC.warn) +
      (r.location ? irow('Location', esc(r.location), IC.pin) : '') + irow('Description', esc(r.description), IC.doc) +
      (r.hasPhoto || r.photo ? irow('Photo', '<div class="rphwrap" data-photo="' + esc(r.id) + '">' + photoHTML(r) + '</div>', IC.photo) : '') +
      irow('Reported by', esc(r.reporterName) + ' <span class="pill r-' + r.reporterRole + '">' + r.reporterRole.toUpperCase() + '</span>' + (sub ? '<br><span class="irow__sub">' + sub + '</span>' : ''), IC.user) +
      irow('Date and time', fmt(r.createdAt), IC.clock) + '</div>';
  }
  /* Stage 3: the admin approves or rejects (with a written reason) */
  function adminReviewHTML(r) {
    var h = '<div class="areview"><b>Admin validation &middot; Stage 3</b><small>The adviser approved this report. Approve it to move it forward, or reject it and tell the reporter why.</small>';
    if (!rejectOpen['a:' + r.id]) return h + '<button class="btn good" data-act="a-ok" data-id="' + r.id + '">Approve</button><button class="btn bad" data-act="a-no" data-id="' + r.id + '">Reject</button></div>';
    return h + '<label for="ra-' + r.id + '">Why are you rejecting this report?</label><textarea id="ra-' + r.id + '" rows="3" placeholder="Write the reason (optional)">' + esc(rjText['ra-' + r.id] || '') + '</textarea>' +
      '<button class="btn bad" data-act="a-no-confirm" data-id="' + r.id + '">Confirm reject</button><button class="btn alt" data-act="a-no-cancel" data-id="' + r.id + '">Cancel</button></div>';
  }
  function focusId(i) { var t = document.getElementById(i); if (t) { t.focus(); } }
  function reviewHTML(r) {
    if (!rejectOpen[r.id]) return '<button class="btn good" data-act="ok" data-id="' + r.id + '">Approve</button><button class="btn bad" data-act="no" data-id="' + r.id + '">Reject</button>';
    return '<label for="rj-' + r.id + '">Why are you rejecting this report?</label><textarea id="rj-' + r.id + '" rows="3" placeholder="Write the reason so the student knows what to fix (optional)">' + esc(rjText['rj-' + r.id] || '') + '</textarea>' +
      '<button class="btn bad" data-act="no-confirm" data-id="' + r.id + '">Confirm reject</button><button class="btn alt" data-act="no-cancel" data-id="' + r.id + '">Cancel</button>';
  }
  function awaitingCount() { return SYNC.on && SYNC.awaiting !== null ? SYNC.awaiting : reports().filter(function (r) { return r.approval === 'pending'; }).length; }
  var flt = { q: '', st: 'all' }, toolsFor = null, lastSig = '';
  /* ----- Reports organised into tabs (Admin, Teacher, Student). Only the open tab's reports are drawn. ----- */
  var rtab = {};
  function rStatus(r) { return r.approval === 'rejected' ? 'rejected' : r.stage === 6 ? 'done' : (r.approval === 'pending' || awaitingAdmin(r)) ? 'wait' : 'prog'; }
  /* tabs = [{k, label, list, alert?, empty?}] : shows the tab the person chose (or the default) and only that tab's reports */
  function tabbed(scope, tabs, def, u, emptyMsg) {
    var cur = rtab[scope], ok = tabs.some(function (t) { return t.k === cur; }); if (!ok) cur = tabs.some(function (t) { return t.k === def; }) ? def : tabs[0].k;
    var bar = '<div class="ctabs rt' + (scope === 'admin' ? ' rt3' : '') + '" role="tablist">' + tabs.map(function (t) {
      return '<button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="' + (t.k === cur) + '" class="ctab' + (t.k === cur ? ' on' : '') + (t.alert && t.list.length ? ' alert' : '') + '" data-act="rtab" data-s="' + scope + '" data-k="' + t.k + '">' + t.label + '<span class="cnt">' + t.list.length + '</span></button>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
    var t0 = tabs.filter(function (t) { return t.k === cur; })[0];
    return bar + (t0.list.length ? t0.list.map(function (r) { return card(r, u); }).join('') : '<p class="note empty">' + (t0.empty || emptyMsg || 'Nothing here yet.') + '</p>');
  }
  /* the person's own reports, grouped by where they are in the process */
  function ownTabs(list, u, emptyMsg) {
    var f = function (k) { return list.filter(function (r) { return rStatus(r) === k; }); };
    return tabbed('own', [
      { k: 'all', label: 'All', list: list, empty: emptyMsg },
      { k: 'wait', label: 'Awaiting approval', list: f('wait'), empty: 'No reports are waiting for approval.' },
      { k: 'prog', label: 'In progress', list: f('prog'), empty: 'No reports are in progress.' },
      { k: 'done', label: 'Accomplished', list: f('done'), empty: 'No accomplished reports yet.' },
      { k: 'rejected', label: 'Rejected', list: f('rejected'), empty: 'No rejected reports.' }
    ], 'all', u, emptyMsg);
  }
  var sec = function (t, list, u, empty) { return '<h3>' + t + '</h3>' + (list.length ? list.map(function (r) { return card(r, u); }).join('') : '<p class="note empty">' + empty + '</p>'); };

  function pendingHTML(list, u) {
    if (!list.length) return '<h3>Waiting for your approval</h3><p class="note empty">No student reports are waiting for you.</p>';
    var by = {}, order = [];
    list.forEach(function (r) { if (!by[r.reporterId]) { by[r.reporterId] = []; order.push(r.reporterId); } by[r.reporterId].push(r); });
    return '<h3>Waiting for your approval</h3>' + order.map(function (sid) {
      var g = by[sid], bar = '';
      if (g.length > 1) bar = '<div class="card box grpbar"><b>' + esc(g[0].reporterName) + '</b><small>' + g.length + ' reports are waiting. Approve or reject them together, or open each one below.</small>' +
        (rejectOpen['all:' + sid]
          ? '<textarea id="rjall-' + esc(sid) + '" rows="3" placeholder="Why are you rejecting these reports? (optional)">' + esc(rjText['rjall-' + sid] || '') + '</textarea><button class="btn bad" data-act="no-all-confirm" data-sid="' + esc(sid) + '">Confirm reject all</button><button class="btn alt" data-act="no-all-cancel" data-sid="' + esc(sid) + '">Cancel</button>'
          : '<button class="btn good" data-act="ok-all" data-sid="' + esc(sid) + '">Approve all (' + g.length + ')</button><button class="btn bad" data-act="no-all" data-sid="' + esc(sid) + '">Reject all</button>') + '</div>';
      return bar + g.map(function (r) { return card(r, u); }).join('');
    }).join('');
  }
  function toolsHTML(u) {
    if (!u || u.role !== 'admin') return '';
    return '<div class="card box"><div class="row"><div><label for="f-q">Search</label><input id="f-q" type="search" placeholder="Title, ID, name, section"></div><div><label for="f-st">Status</label><select id="f-st"><option value="all">All</option>' +
      '<option value="delayed">Delayed</option>' + [1, 3, 4, 5, 6].map(function (n) { return '<option value="' + n + '">' + esc(STAGES[n - 1].t) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div></div></div>' +
      '<details class="card box" id="c-box"><summary><b>Manage site content (Admin)</b></summary><p class="note" id="c-mode">Add a new entry, or press Edit on an entry below to change it.</p><label for="c-t">Section</label><select id="c-t"><option value="grand">Grand Project</option><option value="latest">Latest in ASHS</option><option value="events">Events</option></select>' +
      '<label for="c-n">Title</label><input id="c-n"><label for="c-x">Details <small class="note" id="c-xh"></small></label><input id="c-x"><label for="c-d">Date</label><input id="c-d" type="date">' +
      '<label for="c-p">Photo (optional)</label><input id="c-p" type="file" accept="image/*"><div id="c-pv"></div>' +
      '<div class="row"><button class="btn" data-act="cadd" id="c-go">Add</button><button class="btn alt" data-act="ccancel" id="c-cancel" hidden>Cancel editing</button></div><div id="c-list"></div></details>' +
      '<details class="card box" id="p-box"><summary><b>Manage ASHS Portal (Admin)</b></summary><div id="p-root"></div></details>';
  }
  var CT = { grand: 'Grand Project', latest: 'Latest in ASHS', events: 'Events' }, cedit = null, cimg, cdelArm = null, cTab = 'grand';
  function cpreview() {
    var el = $('#c-pv'); if (!el) return;
    var cur = cimg === undefined ? (cedit ? ((api.content()[cedit.t] || []).filter(function (x) { return x.id === cedit.id; })[0] || {}).img : '') : cimg;
    el.innerHTML = cur ? '<div class="cprev"><img src="' + cur + '" alt="Photo preview"><button class="btn alt" data-act="cphoto-rm">Remove photo</button></div>' : '';
  }
  function cfields() {
    var t = $('#c-t').value, h = $('#c-xh');
    if (h) h.textContent = t === 'events' ? '(time and place, e.g. 8:00 AM \u00B7 ASHS Gym)' : t === 'latest' ? '(short description)' : '(short caption)';
  }
  function cform(on) {
    var go = $('#c-go'), cc = $('#c-cancel'), m = $('#c-mode'); if (!go) return;
    go.textContent = on ? 'Save changes' : 'Add'; cc.hidden = !on; $('#c-t').disabled = !!on;
    m.textContent = on ? 'Editing an entry. Change anything, then press Save changes.' : 'Add a new entry, or press Edit on an entry below to change it.';
    if (!on) { $('#c-n').value = ''; $('#c-x').value = ''; $('#c-d').value = ''; $('#c-p').value = ''; cimg = undefined; cedit = null; }
    cfields(); cpreview();
  }
  function contentList() {
    var c = api.content(), h = '<div class="ctabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Uploaded entries by category">';
    if (!CT[cTab]) cTab = 'grand';
    Object.keys(CT).forEach(function (t) {
      var n = (c[t] || []).length;
      h += '<button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="' + (t === cTab) + '" class="ctab' + (t === cTab ? ' on' : '') + '" data-act="ctab" data-t="' + t + '">' + CT[t] + '<span class="cnt">' + n + '</span></button>';
    });
    h += '</div><div class="cpanel" role="tabpanel">';
    var list = c[cTab] || [];
    if (!list.length) h += '<p class="note empty">Nothing in ' + CT[cTab] + ' yet. Use the form above to add one.</p>';
    list.forEach(function (x) {
      var key = cTab + ':' + x.id, armed = cdelArm === key;
      h += '<div class="crow' + (cedit && cedit.id === x.id ? ' on' : '') + '">' + (x.img ? '<img class="cthumb" src="' + x.img + '" alt="">' : '<span class="cthumb none" aria-hidden="true"></span>') +
        '<div class="cinfo"><b>' + esc(x.title) + '</b><small>' + esc([x.date, x.text].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00B7 ')) + '</small></div>' +
        '<div class="cbtns"><button class="btn alt" data-act="cedit" data-t="' + cTab + '" data-id="' + x.id + '">Edit</button>' +
        '<button class="btn ' + (armed ? 'bad' : 'alt') + '" data-act="cdel" data-t="' + cTab + '" data-id="' + x.id + '">' + (armed ? 'Tap again to remove' : 'Remove') + '</button></div></div>';
    });
    h += '</div>';
    var el = $('#c-list'); if (el) el.innerHTML = h;
  }
  /* ---------- notifications: the red number on the Activity button ---------- */
  var newSet = {}, bdg = null;
  function activityOn() { var v = $('#activity'); return !!v && v.classList.contains('on'); }
  function isAdvisory(u) { return !!u && u.role === 'teacher' && u.advisory; }
  function notifIds(u) {
    if (!u) return [];
    var seen = L('fixit.seen.' + u.id, {});
    return reports().filter(function (r) { return canView(u, r) && r.updatedBy !== u.name && (r.updatedAt || 0) > (seen[r.id] || 0); }).map(function (r) { return r.id; }); /* updates you have not opened yet */
  }
  function markSeen(u) {
    if (!u) return;
    var m = L('fixit.seen.' + u.id, {}), t = Date.now();
    notifIds(u).forEach(function (id) { newSet[id] = 1; m[id] = t; }); S('fixit.seen.' + u.id, m);
  }
  function updateBadge() {
    if (!bdg) return; var c = notifIds(me()).length;
    bdg.hidden = !c; bdg.textContent = c > 99 ? '99+' : String(c);
    bdg.setAttribute('aria-label', c + ' new notification' + (c === 1 ? '' : 's'));
  }
  function renderAct(force) {
    var root = $('#act-root'); if (!root) return; var u = me();
    viewT = !!u && u.role === 'teacher';
    if (u && activityOn()) markSeen(u);
    updateBadge();
    var ae = document.activeElement;
    if (!force && ae && ae.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' && root.contains(ae)) return;   /* never rebuild the page while someone is typing a reason */
    var key = u ? (u.role + ':' + (u.role === 'teacher' ? (u.advisory ? u.section : 'noadv:' + u.id) : u.id)) : 'guest';
    if (toolsFor !== key) {
      toolsFor = key; lastSig = ''; rtab = {};
      root.innerHTML = '<div id="act-tools">' + toolsHTML(u) + '</div><div id="act-list"></div>';
      cedit = null; cimg = undefined; cdelArm = null; if (u && u.role === 'admin') { contentList(); cfields(); if (window.FIXIT_PORTAL) window.FIXIT_PORTAL.mountAdmin(); }
    }
    var h = '';
    if (!u) {
      h = lockedHTML();
    } else if (u.role === 'student') {
      h = ownTabs(api.list(), u, 'You have not sent any reports yet. Open the Report tab to send your first one.');
    } else if (u.role === 'teacher' && u.advisory) {
      var allT = api.list();
      var tPend = allT.filter(function (r) { return r.section === u.section && r.approval === 'pending'; }),
        tRev = allT.filter(function (r) { return r.reporterRole === 'student' && r.section === u.section && r.approval !== 'pending'; }),
        tOwn = allT.filter(function (r) { return r.reporterRole === 'teacher' && r.reporterId === u.id; });
      var tTabs = [['wait', 'Waiting for approval', tPend, true], ['ok', 'Approved', tRev.filter(function (r) { return r.approval !== 'rejected'; })], ['no', 'Rejected', tRev.filter(function (r) { return r.approval === 'rejected'; })], ['own', 'My reports', tOwn]];
      var tCur = tTabs.some(function (t) { return t[0] === rtab.teach; }) ? rtab.teach : 'wait';
      h = '<div class="ctabs rt" role="tablist">' + tTabs.map(function (t) {
        return '<button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="' + (t[0] === tCur) + '" class="ctab' + (t[0] === tCur ? ' on' : '') + (t[3] && t[2].length ? ' alert' : '') + '" data-act="rtab" data-s="teach" data-k="' + t[0] + '">' + t[1] + '<span class="cnt">' + t[2].length + '</span></button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
      if (tCur === 'wait') h += pendingHTML(tPend, u);
      else {
        var tl = tTabs.filter(function (t) { return t[0] === tCur; })[0][2];
        h += tl.length ? tl.map(function (r) { return card(r, u); }).join('') : '<p class="note empty">' + (tCur === 'own' ? 'You have not submitted any reports yet.' : tCur === 'ok' ? 'No approved student reports yet.' : 'No rejected student reports.') + '</p>';
      }
    } else if (u.role === 'teacher') {
      h = ownTabs(api.list(), u, 'You have not submitted any reports yet.') +
        '<p class="note">You are logged in without an advisory section, so there is no approval queue for you.</p>';
    } else {
      var q = flt.q.toLowerCase(), list = api.list().filter(function (r) {
        var okS = flt.st === 'all' || (flt.st === 'delayed' ? r.delayed : r.stage === +flt.st);
        return okS && (!q || [r.title, r.id, r.reporterName, r.section, r.location, r.category].join(' ').toLowerCase().indexOf(q) > -1);
      });
      var aw = awaitingCount();
      h = '<div class="await" role="status" aria-label="' + aw + ' report' + (aw === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' awaiting adviser approval"><div class="await__t"><b>Awaiting adviser</b><small>Display only</small></div><span class="await__n">' + aw + '</span></div>' +
        tabbed('admin', [
          { k: 'need', label: 'Needs my approval', alert: true, list: list.filter(awaitingAdmin), empty: 'No reports are waiting for your approval.' },
          { k: 'prog', label: 'In progress', list: list.filter(function (r) { return r.stage < 6 && r.approval !== 'rejected' && !awaitingAdmin(r); }), empty: 'No reports are in progress.' },
          { k: 'delay', label: 'Delayed', list: list.filter(function (r) { return r.delayed && r.stage !== 6 && r.approval !== 'rejected'; }), empty: 'No delayed reports.' },
          { k: 'done', label: 'Accomplished', list: list.filter(function (r) { return r.stage === 6 && r.approval !== 'rejected'; }), empty: 'No accomplished reports yet.' },
          { k: 'rej', label: 'Rejected', list: list.filter(function (r) { return r.approval === 'rejected'; }), empty: 'No rejected reports.' },
          { k: 'all', label: 'All', list: list, empty: 'No reports match.' }
        ], list.some(awaitingAdmin) ? 'need' : 'all', u, 'No reports match.');
    }
    if (h === lastSig && !force) return; lastSig = h;
    var fid = ae && ae.id, fs = ae && ae.selectionStart, fe = ae && ae.selectionEnd;
    patchList($('#act-list'), h);
    if (force && fid) { var again = document.getElementById(fid); if (again && again !== ae) { again.focus(); try { again.setSelectionRange(fs, fe); } catch (x) { } } }
  }
  /* Only the cards whose content really changed are replaced. Everything else (and the page scroll) stays exactly as it is,
     so the list never jumps back to the top and nothing restarts. */
  function patchList(el, html) {
    var t = document.createElement('div'); t.innerHTML = html;
    var nw = [].slice.call(t.children), old = [].slice.call(el.children), sy = window.pageYOffset, sx = window.pageXOffset;
    nw.forEach(function (n, i) {
      if (!old[i]) el.appendChild(n);
      else if (old[i].outerHTML !== n.outerHTML) el.replaceChild(n, old[i]);
    });
    while (el.children.length > nw.length) el.removeChild(el.lastChild);
    if (window.pageYOffset !== sy || window.pageXOffset !== sx) window.scrollTo(sx, sy);
  }

  /* ---------- Profile: login wizard ---------- */
  function renderAuth() { renderAuthInner(); document.dispatchEvent(new Event('fixit:auth')); }
  var wiz = { step: 'root', section: null, err: '' };
  function resetWiz() { wiz = { step: 'root', section: null, err: '', sc: '', su: '', tr: {}, tl: '', tcode: '', tedit: false, tmsg: '', okMsg: '', sn: '', sr: {} }; }
  function okNote() { return wiz.okMsg ? '<p class="note" role="status" style="color:#15803d;font-weight:600">' + esc(wiz.okMsg) + '</p>' : ''; } /* green message shown on the log-in page after sign-up */
  var WIZ_BACK = { 'teacher-login': 'root', 'teacher-register': 'teacher-login', 'admin': 'root', 'admin-setup': 'root', 'student-login': 'root', 'student-register': 'student-login' };

  function sectionOptions() {
    return '<optgroup label="Grade 12">' + SECTIONS['12'].map(function (s) { return '<option>' + esc(s) + '</option>'; }).join('') +
      '</optgroup><optgroup label="Grade 11">' + SECTIONS['11'].map(function (s) { return '<option>' + esc(s) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>';
  }

  /* Teacher code box on the admin's Profile page: shows the code, with a Change button */
  function teacherCodeCard() {
    var head = '<div class="gcard"><h4>Teacher code</h4><p class="note">Teachers type this code once when they create their own teacher account.</p>';
    if (wiz.tedit) return head + '<label for="tcode-in">New teacher code</label><input id="tcode-in" autocomplete="off" value="' + esc(wiz.tcode || '') + '" maxlength="40">' +
      '<p class="note">6-40 characters: letters, numbers, dot, dash or underscore (no spaces).</p><p class="note" id="tcode-msg" role="alert" style="color:var(--red)"></p>' +
      '<button class="btn" data-act="tcode-save">Save code</button> <button class="btn alt" data-act="tcode-cancel">Cancel</button></div>';
    return head + '<p class="note"><b id="tcode-show" style="font-size:22px;letter-spacing:1px;color:var(--ink)">' + (wiz.tcode ? esc(wiz.tcode) : 'Loading...') + '</b></p>' +
      '<p class="note" id="tcode-msg" role="status" style="color:var(--green,#15803d)">' + esc(wiz.tmsg || '') + '</p>' +
      '<button class="btn" data-act="tcode-edit"' + (wiz.tcode ? '' : ' disabled') + '>Change</button></div>';
  }
  function loadTeacherCode(n) {
    var show = $('#tcode-show'), msg = $('#tcode-msg'); if (!show || wiz.tcode) return;
    /* right after a page refresh the connection to the server is still starting: wait for it (up to ~10 s) instead of showing an error */
    if (!SYNC.on && SYNC.token && location.protocol !== 'file:' && (n || 0) < 20) { setTimeout(function () { loadTeacherCode((n || 0) + 1); }, 500); return; }
    if (!SYNC.on || !SYNC.token) { show.textContent = '-'; msg.style.color = 'var(--red)'; msg.textContent = 'Open the website from the FIXIT server to see or change the code.'; return; }
    SYNC.fetch('/api/admin/teacher-code', { headers: SYNC.hdr(), cache: 'no-store' }, 8000).then(function (r) { return r.json(); }).then(function (j) {
      if (j && j.ok) { wiz.tcode = j.code; if (!wiz.tedit) renderAuth(); }
      else { show.textContent = '-'; msg.style.color = 'var(--red)'; msg.textContent = 'Please log in again.'; }
    }, function () { show.textContent = '-'; msg.style.color = 'var(--red)'; msg.textContent = 'Cannot reach the server.'; });
  }
  function setTrSection(type, section) { /* the section list is only used by teachers who have an advising class */
    var sel = $('#tr-section'); if (!sel) return;
    if (type === 'advising') { sel.disabled = false; sel.innerHTML = '<option value="" selected disabled>Select Section</option>' + sectionOptions(); if (section) sel.value = section; }
    else { sel.disabled = true; sel.innerHTML = '<option value="" selected disabled>' + (type === 'none' ? 'Not needed (no advising class)' : 'Choose "I have an advising class" first') + '</option>'; }
  }
