  /* ---------- my profile: view and edit my OWN account (opened from the account card on the Profile tab) ---------- */
  var pfOpener = null;
  function pfKey(e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') closeProfileEditor(); }
  function closeProfileEditor() {
    var o = $('#pf-ov'); if (o && o.parentNode) o.parentNode.removeChild(o);
    document.body.style.overflow = ''; document.removeEventListener('keydown', pfKey);
    if (pfOpener && pfOpener.focus) { try { pfOpener.focus(); } catch (e) { } } pfOpener = null;
  }
  function pfSections(grade, section) {
    var sel = $('#pf-section'); if (!sel) return;
    sel.innerHTML = (SECTIONS[grade] || []).map(function (x) { return '<option>' + esc(x) + '</option>'; }).join('');
    if (section && (SECTIONS[grade] || []).indexOf(section) > -1) sel.value = section;
  }
  function pfTeacherSection(type, section) {
    var sel = $('#pf-tsection'); if (!sel) return;
    if (type === 'advising') { sel.disabled = false; sel.innerHTML = '<option value="" disabled>Select Section</option>' + sectionOptions(); sel.value = section || ''; }
    else { sel.disabled = true; sel.innerHTML = '<option value="" selected disabled>Not needed (no advising class)</option>'; }
  }
  function openProfileEditor(opener) {
    var u = me(); if (!u) return;
    closeProfileEditor(); pfOpener = opener || null;
    var minPw = u.role === 'student' ? 6 : 8, isA = u.role === 'admin';
    var h = '<p class="note">These are your own account details. Only you can change them.</p>' +
      '<label for="pf-name">Name</label><input id="pf-name" maxlength="' + (isA ? 60 : 100) + '" autocomplete="name"' + (isA ? '' : ' placeholder="Last Name, First Name, Middle Initial"') + '>';
    if (u.role === 'student') {
      h += '<label for="pf-lrn">LRN</label><input id="pf-lrn" readonly>' +
        '<label for="pf-grade">Grade Level</label><select id="pf-grade"><option value="11">Grade 11</option><option value="12">Grade 12</option></select>' +
        '<label for="pf-section">Section</label><select id="pf-section"></select>' +
        '<label for="pf-adviser">Adviser\'s Name</label><input id="pf-adviser" maxlength="100" placeholder="Last Name, First Name, Middle Initial">' +
        '<p class="note">Your LRN cannot be changed.</p>';
    } else if (u.role === 'teacher') {
      h += '<label for="pf-user">Username</label><input id="pf-user" readonly>' +
        '<label for="pf-type">Type of account</label><select id="pf-type"><option value="advising">I have an advising class</option><option value="none">I have no advising class</option></select>' +
        '<label for="pf-tsection">Section you advise</label><select id="pf-tsection"></select>' +
        '<p class="note">Your username cannot be changed. Changing your advising section changes whose reports you review.</p>';
    } else {
      h += '<p class="note">Your admin username cannot be changed.</p>';
    }
    h += '<details class="pfpw"><summary>Change password (optional)</summary>' +
      '<label for="pf-cur">Current password</label><input id="pf-cur" type="password" autocomplete="current-password">' +
      '<label for="pf-new">New password (at least ' + minPw + ' characters)</label><input id="pf-new" type="password" autocomplete="new-password">' +
      '<label for="pf-new2">Repeat new password</label><input id="pf-new2" type="password" autocomplete="new-password"></details>' +
      '<p class="note pferr" id="pf-err" role="alert"></p>' +
      '<button type="button" class="btn" id="pf-save">Save changes</button><button type="button" class="btn alt" id="pf-cancel">Cancel</button>';
    var o = document.createElement('div'); o.id = 'pf-ov'; o.className = 'pfov'; o.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); o.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true'); o.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'pf-title');
    o.innerHTML = '<div class="pfbox"><div class="pfhead"><h3 id="pf-title">My profile</h3><button type="button" class="pfclose" id="pf-x" aria-label="Close">&times;</button></div><div class="pfbody fx">' + h + '</div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(o); document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    $('#pf-name').value = u.name || '';
    if (u.role === 'student') { $('#pf-lrn').value = u.lrn || ''; $('#pf-grade').value = u.grade; pfSections(u.grade, u.section); $('#pf-adviser').value = u.adviser || ''; $('#pf-grade').addEventListener('change', function () { pfSections(this.value, ''); }); }
    if (u.role === 'teacher') { $('#pf-user').value = String(u.id || '').replace(/^teacher:/, ''); $('#pf-type').value = u.advisory ? 'advising' : 'none'; pfTeacherSection(u.advisory ? 'advising' : 'none', u.section); $('#pf-type').addEventListener('change', function () { pfTeacherSection(this.value, ''); }); }
    $('#pf-x').addEventListener('click', closeProfileEditor); $('#pf-cancel').addEventListener('click', closeProfileEditor);
    o.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) { if (e.target === o) closeProfileEditor(); });
    $('#pf-save').addEventListener('click', saveProfileEditor);
    o.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Enter' && e.target.tagName === 'INPUT' && !e.target.readOnly) { e.preventDefault(); saveProfileEditor(); } });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', pfKey);
    setTimeout(function () { var f = $('#pf-name'); if (f) { f.focus(); } }, 30);
  }
  function saveProfileEditor() {
    var u = me(), msg = $('#pf-err'), btn = $('#pf-save'); if (!u) { closeProfileEditor(); return; }
    var p = { name: $('#pf-name').value.trim(), currentPassword: $('#pf-cur').value, newPassword: $('#pf-new').value, newPassword2: $('#pf-new2').value };
    if (u.role === 'student') { p.grade = $('#pf-grade').value; p.section = $('#pf-section').value; p.adviser = $('#pf-adviser').value.trim(); }
    if (u.role === 'teacher') { p.type = $('#pf-type').value; p.section = p.type === 'advising' ? $('#pf-tsection').value : null; }
    var unchanged = p.name === u.name && !p.newPassword && (u.role === 'student' ? (p.grade === u.grade && p.section === u.section && p.adviser === u.adviser)
      : u.role === 'teacher' ? ((p.type === 'advising') === !!u.advisory && (p.section || null) === (u.section || null)) : true);
    if (unchanged) { closeProfileEditor(); toast('No changes to save.'); return; }
    msg.textContent = ''; btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Saving...';
    Promise.resolve(api.updateProfile(p)).then(function (r) {
      if (!r || !r.ok) { var m2 = $('#pf-err'), b2 = $('#pf-save'); if (m2) m2.textContent = (r && r.error) || 'Could not save. Please try again.'; if (b2) { b2.disabled = false; b2.textContent = 'Save changes'; } return; }
      closeProfileEditor(); renderAuth(); renderAct(true); document.dispatchEvent(new Event('fixit:profile')); toast('Profile saved.');
    }, function () { var m3 = $('#pf-err'), b3 = $('#pf-save'); if (m3) m3.textContent = 'Could not save. Please try again.'; if (b3) { b3.disabled = false; b3.textContent = 'Save changes'; } });
  }
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { /* the account card also works with the keyboard */
    if ((e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && e.target && e.target.classList && e.target.classList.contains('acctcard') && e.target.getAttribute('data-act') === 'profile-edit') { e.preventDefault(); openProfileEditor(e.target); }
  });

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target.closest('[data-act]'); if (!b) return; var a = b.dataset.act, id = b.dataset.id, res;
    if (a === 'photo-zoom') { b.classList.toggle('zoom'); return; }
    if (a === 'goprof') $('nav button[data-v="profile"]').click();
    else if (a === 'profile-edit') openProfileEditor(b);
    else if (a === 'logout') { api.logout(); document.dispatchEvent(new Event('fixit:clear-search')); resetWiz(); renderAuth(); renderAct(true); toast('Logged out'); }
    else if (a === 'prof-teacher') { wiz.step = 'teacher-login'; wiz.err = ''; renderAuth(); }
    else if (a === 'prof-admin') {
      wiz.err = '';
      var openAdmin = function (setup) { wiz.step = setup ? 'admin-setup' : 'admin'; renderAuth(); };
      if (!SYNC.on) { openAdmin(false); return; }
      SYNC.fetch('/api/admin/status', { cache: 'no-store' }, 8000).then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
        .then(function (j) { openAdmin(!!(j && j.setupNeeded)); }, function () { openAdmin(false); });
    }
    else if (a === 'prof-back') { wiz.step = WIZ_BACK[wiz.step] || 'root'; wiz.err = ''; wiz.okMsg = ''; wiz.sr = {}; renderAuth(); }
    else if (a === 'tcode-edit') { wiz.tedit = true; wiz.tmsg = ''; renderAuth(); var ti2 = $('#tcode-in'); if (ti2) { ti2.focus(); ti2.select(); } }
    else if (a === 'tcode-cancel') { wiz.tedit = false; renderAuth(); }
    else if (a === 'tcode-save') {
      var tin = $('#tcode-in'), tmsg = $('#tcode-msg'); if (!tin || !tmsg) return;
      SYNC.post('/api/admin/teacher-code', { code: tin.value.trim() }).then(function (j) {
        if (!j.ok) { tmsg.textContent = j.error || 'Could not save.'; return; }
        wiz.tcode = j.code; wiz.tedit = false; wiz.tmsg = 'Saved. Teachers must now use this code.'; renderAuth();
      });
    }
    else if (a === 'teacher-register-open') { wiz.step = 'teacher-register'; wiz.err = ''; renderAuth(); }
    else if (a === 'teacher-login-go') {
      wiz.tl = $('#tl-u').value;
      Promise.resolve(api.loginTeacher(wiz.tl, $('#tl-p').value)).then(function (r1) {
        if (!r1.ok) { wiz.err = r1.error; renderAuth(); return; }
        resetWiz(); renderAuth(); renderAct(true); toast('Logged in'); if (!r1.advisory) $('nav button[data-v="home"]').click();
      });
    } else if (a === 'teacher-register-go') {
      var tv = function (i) { return $('#' + i).value; };
      wiz.tr = { type: tv('tr-type'), section: tv('tr-section'), name: tv('tr-name'), username: tv('tr-user'), code: tv('tr-code') };
      Promise.resolve(api.registerTeacher({ type: wiz.tr.type, section: wiz.tr.section, name: wiz.tr.name, username: wiz.tr.username, password: tv('tr-pw'), password2: tv('tr-pw2'), code: wiz.tr.code })).then(function (r2) {
        if (!r2.ok) { wiz.err = r2.error; renderAuth(); return; }
        var un = wiz.tr.username; resetWiz(); wiz.step = 'teacher-login'; wiz.tl = un; wiz.okMsg = 'Account created! Now log in with your username and password.'; renderAuth(); toast('Account created. Please log in.');
      });
    } else if (a === 'admin-login') {
      api.loginAdmin($('#l-u').value, $('#l-p').value).then(function (r) {
        if (!r.ok && /not set up/i.test(r.error || '')) { wiz.step = 'admin-setup'; wiz.err = ''; renderAuth(); return; }
        if (!r.ok) { wiz.err = r.error; renderAuth(); return; }
        resetWiz(); renderAuth(); renderAct(true); toast('Logged in');
      });
    }
    else if (a === 'admin-setup-go') {
      var sc = $('#as-c').value.trim(), su = $('#as-u').value.trim(), sp = $('#as-p').value, sp2 = $('#as-p2').value;
      wiz.sc = sc; wiz.su = su;
      if (!SYNC.on) { wiz.err = 'Open the website from the FIXIT server (http://localhost:3000) to create the admin account.'; renderAuth(); return; }
      if (!sc || !su || !sp) { wiz.err = 'Please fill in every box.'; renderAuth(); return; }
      if (sp.length < 8) { wiz.err = 'Password needs at least 8 characters.'; renderAuth(); return; }
      if (sp !== sp2) { wiz.err = 'Passwords do not match.'; renderAuth(); return; }
      SYNC.post('/api/admin/setup', { code: sc, username: su, password: sp }).then(function (r) {
        if (!r.ok) { wiz.err = r.error || 'Something went wrong.'; renderAuth(); return; }
        api.loginAdmin(su, sp).then(function (r2) {
          if (!r2.ok) { wiz.step = 'admin'; wiz.err = 'Admin account created. Please log in.'; renderAuth(); return; }
          resetWiz(); renderAuth(); renderAct(true); toast('Admin account created');
        });
      });
    }
    else if (a === 'prof-student') { wiz.step = 'student-login'; wiz.err = ''; renderAuth(); }
    else if (a === 'prof-register') { wiz.step = 'student-register'; wiz.err = ''; renderAuth(); }
    else if (a === 'student-login-go') {
      api.loginStudent($('#sl-n').value, $('#sl-p').value).then(function (r) {
        if (!r.ok) { wiz.err = r.error; renderAuth(); return; }
        resetWiz(); renderAuth(); renderAct(true); toast('Logged in');
      });
    }
    else if (a === 'student-register-go') {
      var v = function (i) { return $('#' + i).value; };
      wiz.sr = { lrn: v('sr-lrn'), name: v('sr-name'), grade: v('sr-grade'), section: v('sr-section'), adviser: v('sr-adviser') };
      api.registerStudent({ lrn: v('sr-lrn'), name: v('sr-name'), grade: v('sr-grade'), section: v('sr-section'), adviser: v('sr-adviser'), password: v('sr-pw'), password2: v('sr-pw2') }).then(function (r) {
        if (!r.ok) { wiz.err = r.error; renderAuth(); return; }
        var ln = v('sr-lrn').replace(/\s/g, ''); resetWiz(); wiz.step = 'student-login'; wiz.sn = ln; wiz.okMsg = 'Account created! Now log in with your LRN and password.'; renderAuth(); toast('Account created. Please log in.');
      });
    }
    else if (a === 'ok-all') { res = api.decideMany(b.dataset.sid, true, ''); if (res.ok) toast('Approved ' + res.count + ' reports'); }
    else if (a === 'no-all') { rejectOpen['all:' + b.dataset.sid] = true; renderAct(true); focusId('rjall-' + b.dataset.sid); return; }
    else if (a === 'no-all-cancel') { delete rejectOpen['all:' + b.dataset.sid]; renderAct(true); return; }
    else if (a === 'no-all-confirm') {
      var ta2 = $('#rjall-' + b.dataset.sid); res = api.decideMany(b.dataset.sid, false, ta2 ? ta2.value.trim() : '');
      delete rejectOpen['all:' + b.dataset.sid]; if (res.ok) toast('Rejected ' + res.count + ' reports');
    }
    else if (a === 'no') { rejectOpen[id] = true; renderAct(true); focusId('rj-' + id); return; }
    else if (a === 'a-ok') { res = api.adminDecide(id, true, ''); if (res.ok) toast('Report approved'); }
    else if (a === 'a-no') { rejectOpen['a:' + id] = true; renderAct(true); focusId('ra-' + id); return; }
    else if (a === 'a-no-cancel') { delete rejectOpen['a:' + id]; renderAct(true); return; }
    else if (a === 'a-no-confirm') { var ta3 = $('#ra-' + id); res = api.adminDecide(id, false, ta3 ? ta3.value.trim() : ''); delete rejectOpen['a:' + id]; if (res.ok) toast('Report rejected'); }
    else if (a === 'no-cancel') { delete rejectOpen[id]; renderAct(true); return; }
    else if (a === 'no-confirm') { var ta = $('#rj-' + id); res = api.decide(id, false, ta ? ta.value.trim() : ''); delete rejectOpen[id]; if (res.ok) toast('Report rejected'); }
    else if (a === 'ok') res = api.decide(id, true);
    else if (a === 'stage') res = api.setStage(id, +b.dataset.n);
    else if (a === 'delay') res = api.setDelay(id, b.dataset.on === '1');
    else if (a === 'cadd') {
      var t = $('#c-t').value, ti = $('#c-n').value.trim(); if (!ti) { toast('Enter a title'); return; }
      var fields = { title: ti, text: $('#c-x').value.trim(), date: $('#c-d').value };
      if (cedit) { fields.img = cimg; res = api.contentEdit(cedit.t, cedit.id, fields); }
      else { if (cimg) fields.img = cimg; res = api.contentAdd(t, fields); }
      if (res.ok) { toast(cedit ? 'Changes saved' : 'Added'); cTab = cedit ? cedit.t : t; cdelArm = null; cform(false); contentList(); } else toast(res.error);
      return;
    }
    else if (a === 'cedit') {
      var it = (api.content()[b.dataset.t] || []).filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0]; if (!it) return;
      cedit = { t: b.dataset.t, id: id }; cTab = b.dataset.t; cimg = undefined; cdelArm = null;
      $('#c-t').value = b.dataset.t; $('#c-n').value = it.title || ''; $('#c-x').value = it.text || ''; $('#c-d').value = it.date || ''; $('#c-p').value = '';
      cform(true); contentList(); var bx = $('#c-n'); if (bx) { bx.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' }); bx.focus(); }
      return;
    }
    else if (a === 'ccancel') { cdelArm = null; cform(false); contentList(); return; }
    else if (a === 'rtab') { rtab[b.dataset.s] = b.dataset.k; renderAct(true); return; }
    else if (a === 'ctab') { cTab = b.dataset.t; cdelArm = null; contentList(); return; }
    else if (a === 'cphoto-rm') { cimg = ''; $('#c-p').value = ''; cpreview(); return; }
    else if (a === 'cdel') {
      var ck = b.dataset.t + ':' + id;
      if (cdelArm !== ck) { cdelArm = ck; contentList(); setTimeout(function () { if (cdelArm === ck) { cdelArm = null; contentList(); } }, 4000); return; }
      cdelArm = null; res = api.contentDel(b.dataset.t, id);
      if (res.ok) { if (cedit && cedit.id === id) cform(false); toast('Removed'); contentList(); return; }
    }
    if (res) { if (!res.ok) toast(res.error); renderAct(true); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    if (e.target.id === 'c-t') cfields();
    if (e.target.id !== 'c-p') return;
    var f = e.target.files && e.target.files[0]; if (!f) return;
    if (!/^image\//.test(f.type)) { toast('Please choose an image'); e.target.value = ''; return; }
    var rd = new FileReader();
    rd.onload = function () {
      var im = new Image();
      im.onload = function () { /* shrink so it fits in browser storage */
        var m = 900, k = Math.min(1, m / Math.max(im.width, im.height)), cv = document.createElement('canvas');
        cv.width = Math.round(im.width * k); cv.height = Math.round(im.height * k);
        var cx = cv.getContext('2d'); cx.fillStyle = '#fff'; cx.fillRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height); cx.drawImage(im, 0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);
        cimg = cv.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.72); cpreview();
      };
      im.onerror = function () { toast('That image could not be read'); };
      im.src = rd.result;
    };
    rd.readAsDataURL(f);
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) { var i = e.target.id || ''; if (i.indexOf('rj-') === 0 || i.indexOf('rjall-') === 0 || i.indexOf('ra-') === 0) rjText[i] = e.target.value; });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) { if (e.target.id === 'f-q') { flt.q = e.target.value; renderAct(true); } });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) { if (e.target.id === 'f-st') { flt.st = e.target.value; renderAct(true); } });
  document.addEventListener('toggle', function (e) { var d = e.target; if (d.classList && d.classList.contains('rc')) { if (d.open) { openIds[d.dataset.id] = 1; delete PHOTO.fail[d.dataset.id]; if (d.querySelector('[data-photo]')) loadPhoto(d.dataset.id); } else delete openIds[d.dataset.id]; } }, true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key !== 'Enter') return;
    var id = e.target.id;
    if (id === 'sl-n' || id === 'sl-p') { e.preventDefault(); $('[data-act="student-login-go"]').click(); }
    else if (id === 'sr-pw2') { e.preventDefault(); $('[data-act="student-register-go"]').click(); }
    else if (id === 'l-u' || id === 'l-p') { e.preventDefault(); $('[data-act="admin-login"]').click(); }
    else if (id === 'as-c' || id === 'as-u' || id === 'as-p' || id === 'as-p2') { e.preventDefault(); $('[data-act="admin-setup-go"]').click(); }
    else if (id === 'tcode-in') { e.preventDefault(); $('[data-act="tcode-save"]').click(); }
    else if (id === 'tl-u' || id === 'tl-p') { e.preventDefault(); $('[data-act="teacher-login-go"]').click(); }
    else if (id === 'tr-pw2' || id === 'tr-code') { e.preventDefault(); $('[data-act="teacher-register-go"]').click(); }
  });

  /* sign-up form: the section list follows the chosen grade level */
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) { if (e.target.id === 'tr-type') setTrSection(e.target.value, ''); });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    if (e.target.id !== 'sr-grade') return;
    var sel = $('#sr-section'); sel.disabled = false;
    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="" selected disabled>Select Section</option>' + SECTIONS[e.target.value].map(function (s) { return '<option>' + esc(s) + '</option>'; }).join('');
  });
  $('nav button[data-v="activity"]').addEventListener('click', function () { renderAct(true); });

  /* ----- Admin management sections go back to their default state ----- */
  function siteDirty() { return !!$('#c-box') && !!(cedit || cimg || ($('#c-n') && $('#c-n').value.trim()) || ($('#c-x') && $('#c-x').value.trim()) || ($('#c-d') && $('#c-d').value) || ($('#c-p') && $('#c-p').value)); }
  function portalDirty() { return !!(window.FIXIT_PORTAL && window.FIXIT_PORTAL.isDirty && window.FIXIT_PORTAL.isDirty()); }
  function resetSite(keepOpen) { var b = $('#c-box'); if (!b) return; cform(false); cTab = 'grand'; cdelArm = null; contentList(); if (!keepOpen) b.open = false; }
  function resetPortal(keepOpen) { var b = $('#p-box'); if (!b) return; if (window.FIXIT_PORTAL && window.FIXIT_PORTAL.reset) window.FIXIT_PORTAL.reset(); if (!keepOpen) b.open = false; }
  /* leaving the Activity page: ask first when something is half-edited, otherwise reset quietly */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var nb = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('nav button'); if (!nb || nb.dataset.v === 'activity' || !activityOn() || !$('#c-box')) return;
    var sd = siteDirty(), pd = portalDirty();
    if ((sd || pd) && !window.confirm('You have unsaved changes in ' + (sd && pd ? 'Manage Site Content and Manage ASHS Portal' : sd ? 'Manage Site Content' : 'Manage ASHS Portal') + '.\n\nLeave and discard them?')) { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); return; }
    resetSite(); resetPortal();
  }, true);
  /* opening a management section shows it in its default state and resets the other one (unless it has unsaved edits) */
  document.addEventListener('toggle', function (e) {
    var d = e.target; if (!d || !d.open || (d.id !== 'c-box' && d.id !== 'p-box')) return;
    if (d.id === 'p-box') { if (!portalDirty()) resetPortal(true); if (!siteDirty()) resetSite(); }
    else { if (!siteDirty()) resetSite(true); if (!portalDirty()) resetPortal(); }
  }, true);

  renderAuth(); renderAct(true);
  setInterval(function () { renderAct(false); }, 3000); /* refresh every 3 s: changes in another tab reach this one quickly */
  window.addEventListener('storage', function () { renderAct(false); });
  (function () {
    var ab = document.querySelector('nav button[data-v="activity"]'); if (!ab) return;
    bdg = document.createElement('span'); bdg.className = 'nbadge'; bdg.hidden = true; bdg.setAttribute('role', 'status'); ab.appendChild(bdg);
    document.querySelectorAll('nav button').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () {
        newSet = {}; if (b === ab) { markSeen(me()); renderAct(true); } updateBadge();
      });
    });
    document.addEventListener('fixit:auth', updateBadge); updateBadge();
  })();
  api.me = me; api.refresh = function () { renderAct(true); };
  window.FIXIT_API = api; /* single seam to replace with real server calls */
  SYNC.start();
})();

/* ===== ASHS Portal: Academic Guide, SBMS link, Persons In Charge (added) =====
   Everything here is stored in the shared homepage content (content.portal) and is edited by the admin on the Activity page
   (Manage ASHS Portal). The lists below are only the starting values used until the admin saves a change. */
(function () {
  var $ = function (s, r) { return (r || document).querySelector(s); }, $$ = function (s, r) { return [].slice.call((r || document).querySelectorAll(s)); };
  var esc = function (s) { var d = document.createElement('div'); d.textContent = s == null ? '' : String(s); return d.innerHTML; };
  var say = function (m) { var t = $('#toast'); if (!t) return; t.textContent = m; t.classList.add('on'); setTimeout(function () { t.classList.remove('on'); }, 3200); };
  var uid = function () { return 'p' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5); };
  var clone = function (x) { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x)); };

  /* floors are edited as plain text:  a line with the word "floor" starts a floor, other lines are rooms ("101 - Newton" or just a name) */
  function parseFloors(text) {
    var floors = [], cur = null;
    String(text || '').split(/\r?\n/).forEach(function (raw) {
      var ln = raw.trim(); if (!ln) return;
      if (/floor/i.test(ln) && !/\s[-–—|]\s/.test(ln)) { cur = { label: ln.replace(/:$/, '').trim(), rooms: [] }; floors.push(cur); return; }
      if (!cur) { cur = { label: '', rooms: [] }; floors.push(cur); }
      var m = /^(.*?)\s[-–—|]\s(.+)$/.exec(ln);
      cur.rooms.push(m ? { tag: m[1].trim(), name: m[2].trim() } : { tag: '', name: ln });
    });
    return floors;
  }
  function floorsText(floors) {
    return (floors || []).map(function (f) { return (f.label ? f.label + '\n' : '') + (f.rooms || []).map(function (r) { return r.tag ? r.tag + ' - ' + r.name : r.name; }).join('\n'); }).join('\n\n');
  }
  function bld(name, text) { return { name: name, floors: parseFloors(text) }; }

  var DEF = {
    sbms: { url: 'https://sites.google.com/deped.gov.ph/ashs342235/e-sbm/curriculum-and-teaching/contextualized-learning-materials' },
    persons: [],
    guide: [
      { id: 'enroll', type: 'steps', title: 'Enrollment Process', heading: 'Enrollment Guide',
        intro: 'For incoming Grade 11 and Grade 12 students. Prepare your documents and follow the enrollment steps.',
        cards: [
          { title: 'Grade 11 — New Students', steps: ['Prepare your Grade 10 Report Card (SF9).', 'Bring a photocopy of your PSA Birth Certificate, if requested or not yet on file.', 'Prepare a Certificate of Good Moral Character, if required.', 'Bring your Certificate of Completion, if available or requested.', 'Prepare 2×2 ID photos and an envelope, if required by the school.', 'Get and complete the school enrollment form.', 'Submit your documents to the registrar. Confirm your strand, schedule, and enrollment status.'], noteTitle: '', note: '' },
          { title: 'Grade 12 — Returning ASHS Students', steps: ['Prepare your Grade 11 Report Card (SF9).', 'Ask whether an enrollment form or updated student information is also needed.', "Submit your report card and complete the school's enrollment process."], noteTitle: 'Important', note: 'For Grade 12 returning students, only the report card may be needed. However, this is not a guaranteed requirement. Please confirm with the school registrar, as the requirements may change.' }
        ],
        foot: 'Prepare your documents and follow the enrollment steps.\nCheck with ASHS for the latest requirements and dates.' },
      { id: 'buildings', type: 'buildings', title: 'Buildings & Sections', heading: 'Buildings & Sections',
        intro: "Find your section's room by building and floor, or search by room number or section name.",
        search: 'Search a room or section (e.g. Newton, 205)',
        buildings: [
          bld('EBD Building', "1st Floor\nCare - Care Center\nClinic - School Clinic\n101 - Luminaries\n102 - Pathfinders\n103 - Practitioners\n104 - Innovators\n\n2nd Floor\nOffice - Principal's Office\nOffice - Admin Office\nOffice - Faculty\nLibrary - School Library\n205 - Elton Mayo\n206 - Philip Crosby\n\n3rd Floor\nArchive - School Property / Archived\n312 - Douglas McGregor\n313 - Luca Pacioli\n314 - Developers\n315 - Thinkers"),
          bld('DepEd 1 Building', '1st Floor\n101 - Descartes\n102 - Einstein\n103 - Euclid\n104 - Euler\n\n2nd Floor\n205 - Fibonacci\n206 - Laplace\n207 - Leibniz\n208 - Newton\n\n3rd Floor\n309 - Mencius\n310 - Shakespeare\n311 - Pascal\n312 - Pearson\n\n4th Floor\n413 - Aquinas\n414 - Aurelius\n415 - Confucius\n416 - Freud'),
          bld('DepEd 2 Building', '1st Floor\n101 - Advocates\n102 - Ambassadors\n103 - Analysts\n\n2nd Floor\n204 - Aspirers\n205 - Builders\n206 - Catalysts\n\n3rd Floor\n307 - Conquerors\n308 - Designers\n309 - Voyagers\n\n4th Floor\n410 - Diplomats\n411 - Empiricists\n412 - Strategists')
        ] }
    ]
  };

  function home() { return window.FIXIT_HOME; }
  function portal() {
    var c = home() ? home().get() : {}, p = (c && c.portal) || {};
    return {
      sbms: p.sbms && typeof p.sbms === 'object' ? p.sbms : clone(DEF.sbms),
      guide: Array.isArray(p.guide) ? p.guide : clone(DEF.guide),
      persons: normPersons(Array.isArray(p.persons) ? p.persons : clone(DEF.persons)),
      org: p.org && typeof p.org === 'object' ? { mode: p.org.mode === 'upload' && p.org.file ? 'upload' : 'manual', file: p.org.file || '', kind: p.org.kind || '', name: p.org.name || '' } : { mode: 'manual' }
    };
  }
  function savePortal(p) {
    var c = home().get(); var org = { mode: p.org.mode }; if (p.org.file) { org.file = p.org.file; org.kind = p.org.kind; if (p.org.name) org.name = p.org.name; }
    c.portal = { sbms: p.sbms, guide: p.guide, persons: p.persons, org: org };
    var ok = home().save(c);
    renderPublic(); return ok;
  }
  var safeUrl = function (u) { u = String(u || '').trim(); return /^https?:\/\/[^\s<>"]+$/i.test(u) ? u : ''; };

  /* ---------------- public pages ---------------- */
  var activeTab = null;
  function lines(t) { return esc(t).replace(/\n/g, '<br>'); }
  function paneHTML(s) {
    var h = '<h2 class="lg">' + esc(s.heading || s.title) + '</h2>' + (s.intro ? '<p class="acsub">' + esc(s.intro) + '</p>' : '');
    if (s.type === 'buildings') {
      h += '<div class="bsearch"><input type="search" class="acsearch" placeholder="' + esc(s.search || 'Search a room or section') + '" aria-label="Search a room or section"></div><div class="bldgrid">';
      (s.buildings || []).forEach(function (b) {
        h += '<article class="bld"><h3>' + esc(b.name) + '</h3><div class="bbody">';
        (b.floors || []).forEach(function (f) {
          h += '<div class="fl">' + (f.label ? '<h4>' + esc(f.label) + '</h4>' : '') + '<ul>';
          (f.rooms || []).forEach(function (r) {
            var num = /^\d+$/.test(r.tag || '');
            h += '<li><span class="rm ' + (num ? 'gold' : 'blue') + '">' + esc(r.tag || '•') + '</span><span class="rn">' + esc(r.name) + '</span></li>';
          });
          h += '</ul></div>';
        });
        h += '</div></article>';
      });
      h += '</div><p class="acnone" hidden>No room or section matches your search.</p>';
    } else {
      h += '<div class="encols' + ((s.cards || []).length === 1 ? ' one' : '') + '">';
      (s.cards || []).forEach(function (c) {
        h += '<article class="enc"><h3>' + esc(c.title) + '</h3><ol>' + (c.steps || []).map(function (x) { return '<li>' + esc(x) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ol>' +
          (c.note ? '<div class="imp">' + (c.noteTitle ? '<b>' + esc(c.noteTitle) + '</b>' : '') + '<p>' + lines(c.note) + '</p></div>' : '') + '</article>';
      });
      h += '</div>' + (s.foot ? '<p class="acfoot">' + lines(s.foot) + '</p>' : '');
    }
    return h;
  }
  function renderGuide() {
    var tabs = $('#actabs'), panes = $('#acpanes'); if (!tabs || !panes) return;
    var g = portal().guide.filter(function (s) { return s && s.title; });
    if (!g.some(function (s) { return s.id === activeTab; })) activeTab = g.length ? g[0].id : null;
    tabs.hidden = !g.length;
    tabs.innerHTML = g.map(function (s) { return '<button type="button" role="tab" class="' + (s.id === activeTab ? 'on' : '') + '" aria-selected="' + (s.id === activeTab) + '" data-tab="' + esc(s.id) + '">' + esc(s.title) + '</button>'; }).join('');
    panes.innerHTML = g.length ? g.map(function (s) { return '<div class="acpane' + (s.id === activeTab ? ' on' : '') + '" data-pane="' + esc(s.id) + '">' + paneHTML(s) + '</div>'; }).join('') : '<p class="acnone">The Academic Guide has no sections yet.</p>';
  }
  function renderSbms() {
    var a = $('#sbmsBtn'); if (!a) return; var u = safeUrl(portal().sbms.url);
    if (u) { a.href = u; a.target = '_blank'; a.rel = 'noopener noreferrer'; } else { a.href = '#'; a.removeAttribute('target'); }
  }
  function renderPublic() { renderGuide(); renderPersons(); renderSbms(); var pr = $('#p-root'); if (pr && !ui.sec && !ui.pf && !ui.busy && !pr.contains(document.activeElement)) fillAdmin(); }

  /* ---------------- organizational chart (public display, also used for the admin preview) ---------------- */
  var fileOk = function (f) { f = String(f || ''); return /^[a-f0-9]{24}\.(png|jpg|pdf)$/.test(f) || /^data:(image\/(png|jpeg|webp)|application\/pdf);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+\/=]+$/.test(f); };
  var isPdf = function (f) { return /\.pdf$/.test(f) || /^data:application\/pdf/.test(f); };
  var furl = function (f) { return /^data:/.test(f) ? f : '/api/portal-file/' + encodeURIComponent(f); };
  var initials = function (n) { return String(n || '').replace(/\b(dr|mr|mrs|ms|sir|maam|engr)\.?\s/ig, '').trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 2).map(function (w) { return w.charAt(0); }).join('').toUpperCase(); };

  /* persons: make sure every person has a valid "reports to" (older data only had a level) and that nobody reports to themself or in a loop */
  function normPersons(list) {
    var a = (Array.isArray(list) ? list : []).filter(function (x) { return x && typeof x === 'object' && x.name; }).map(function (x) { return Object.assign({}, x); });
    var legacy = !a.some(function (x) { return typeof x.parent === 'string'; });
    if (legacy) a.forEach(function (x, i) {
      var l = +x.level || 1, best = -1; x.parent = '';
      if (l > 1) { for (var k = 0; k < a.length; k++) { if (k !== i && (+a[k].level || 1) < l) { if (k < i) best = k; else if (best < 0) { best = k; break; } } } if (best > -1) x.parent = a[best].id; }
    });
    var ids = {}; a.forEach(function (x) { ids[x.id] = x; });
    a.forEach(function (x) { if (typeof x.parent !== 'string' || x.parent === x.id || !ids[x.parent]) x.parent = ''; });
    a.forEach(function (x) { var c = x, n = 0; while (c.parent && n++ < 500) { if (c.parent === x.id) { x.parent = ''; break; } c = ids[c.parent]; } });
    return a;
  }
  function kidsOf(list) { var k = {}; list.forEach(function (x) { (k[x.parent || ''] = k[x.parent || ''] || []).push(x); }); return k; }
  function ordered(list) { /* the people in chart order (each person followed by their team) with their depth */
    var k = kidsOf(list), out = [];
    (function walk(pid, d) { (k[pid] || []).forEach(function (x) { out.push({ x: x, d: d }); walk(x.id, d + 1); }); })('', 0);
    return out;
  }
  function descendants(list, id) { var k = kidsOf(list), out = {}; (function w(pid) { (k[pid] || []).forEach(function (x) { out[x.id] = 1; w(x.id); }); })(id); return out; }
  function relevel(list) { ordered(list).forEach(function (o) { o.x.level = Math.min(6, o.d + 1); }); }

  function chartCard(x, d) {
    var pic = x.photo && fileOk(x.photo) && !isPdf(x.photo) ? '<img class="oph" src="' + esc(furl(x.photo)) + '" alt="' + esc(x.name) + '" loading="lazy">' : '<span class="oph oini" aria-hidden="true">' + esc(initials(x.name)) + '</span>';
    return '<div class="ocard d' + Math.min(d, 4) + '">' + pic + '<b>' + esc(x.name) + '</b>' + (x.role ? '<small>' + esc(x.role) + '</small>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function treeHTML(list) {
    var k = kidsOf(list);
    function node(x, d) {
      var ch = k[x.id] || [], hang = ch.length >= 5 && ch.every(function (c) { return !(k[c.id] || []).length; }); /* a long team of people with no team of their own hangs in a tidy column */
      return '<li' + (hang ? ' class="hang"' : '') + '>' + chartCard(x, d) + (ch.length ? '<ul' + (hang ? ' class="ohang"' : '') + '>' + ch.map(function (c) { return node(c, d + 1); }).join('') + '</ul>' : '') + '</li>';
    }
    return '<div class="oscroll"><div class="otree"><ul class="oroot">' + (k[''] || []).map(function (x) { return node(x, 0); }).join('') + '</ul></div></div>';
  }
  function fileHTML(org) {
    var u = esc(furl(org.file));
    if (isPdf(org.file)) {
      return '<div class="orgfile pdf"><object data="' + u + '" type="application/pdf" aria-label="Organizational chart (PDF)"><p class="acnone">Your device cannot show the PDF inside the page.</p></object>' +
        '<p class="orgopen"><a href="' + u + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Open the organizational chart (PDF)</a></p></div>';
    }
    return '<figure class="orgfile"><a href="' + u + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><img src="' + u + '" alt="Organizational chart of Alangilan Senior High School"></a><figcaption>Tap the chart to open it full size.</figcaption></figure>';
  }
  function centerChart() { /* on a narrow screen the chart is wider than the page: start with the top person in the middle */
    var sc = $('#orgchart .oscroll'), card = sc && $('.oroot > li > .ocard', sc); if (!sc || !card || sc.scrollWidth <= sc.clientWidth) return;
    var c = card.getBoundingClientRect(), r = sc.getBoundingClientRect(); sc.scrollLeft += (c.left + c.width / 2) - (r.left + r.width / 2);
  }
  function renderPersons() {
    var el = $('#orgchart'); if (!el) return;
    var p = portal();
    if (p.org.mode === 'upload' && p.org.file && fileOk(p.org.file)) { el.innerHTML = fileHTML(p.org); return; }
    if (!p.persons.length) { el.innerHTML = '<p class="acnone">The organizational chart is being updated. Please check back soon.</p>'; return; }
    el.innerHTML = treeHTML(p.persons) + '<p class="orghint">On a small screen, swipe sideways to see the whole chart.</p>';
    if (el.offsetParent) centerChart();
  }

  /* ---------------- public wiring ---------------- */
  var showV = function (id) { if (typeof showView === 'function') showView(id); };
  function wire() {
    var a = $('#acadBtn'), p = $('#picBtn'), s = $('#sbmsBtn');
    if (a) a.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); showV('acad'); });
    if (p) p.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); showV('persons'); setTimeout(centerChart, 60); });
    if (s) s.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (!safeUrl(portal().sbms.url)) { e.preventDefault(); say('The SBMS link has not been set yet.'); } });
    var b1 = $('#acback'), b2 = $('#pcback');
    if (b1) b1.onclick = function () { showV('home'); }; if (b2) b2.onclick = function () { showV('home'); };
    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var t = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#actabs [data-tab]'); if (!t) return;
      activeTab = t.getAttribute('data-tab'); renderGuide();
    });
    document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
      if (!e.target.classList || !e.target.classList.contains('acsearch')) return;
      var pane = e.target.closest('.acpane'), q = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase(), any = false;
      $$('.bld', pane).forEach(function (b) {
        var bs = false;
        $$('.fl', b).forEach(function (f) {
          var fs = false;
          $$('li', f).forEach(function (li) { var m = !q || li.textContent.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) > -1; li.style.display = m ? '' : 'none'; if (m) fs = true; });
          f.style.display = fs ? '' : 'none'; if (fs) bs = true;
        });
        b.style.display = bs ? '' : 'none'; if (bs) any = true;
      });
      var none = $('.acnone', pane); if (none) none.hidden = any;
    });
  }

  /* ---------------- admin panel ---------------- */
  var ui = { hub: '', sec: null, isNew: false, pf: null, pm: '', armed: null, busy: false, focus: null };
  var SVG = {
    guide: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2 9l10-5 10 5-10 5z"/><path d="M6 11.5V16c0 1.5 2.7 3 6 3s6-1.5 6-3v-4.5"/></svg>',
    sbms: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 14a4 4 0 0 0 5.7 0l3-3a4 4 0 0 0-5.7-5.7l-1 1"/><path d="M14 10a4 4 0 0 0-5.7 0l-3 3a4 4 0 0 0 5.7 5.7l1-1"/></svg>',
    persons: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="9" y="3" width="6" height="5" rx="1"/><rect x="2" y="16" width="6" height="5" rx="1"/><rect x="16" y="16" width="6" height="5" rx="1"/><path d="M12 8v4M5 16v-4h14v4"/></svg>'
  };
  function fld(label, html) { return '<label>' + label + '</label>' + html; }
  function inp(cls, val, ph) { return '<input class="' + cls + '" value="' + esc(val) + '"' + (ph ? ' placeholder="' + esc(ph) + '"' : '') + '>'; }
  function ta(cls, val, rows, ph) { return '<textarea class="' + cls + '" rows="' + (rows || 3) + '"' + (ph ? ' placeholder="' + esc(ph) + '"' : '') + '>' + esc(val) + '</textarea>'; }
  function pbtn(act, label, extra, cls) { return '<button type="button" class="btn ' + (cls || 'alt') + '" data-pa="' + act + '"' + (extra || '') + '>' + label + '</button>'; }
  function di(i, j, k) { return (i !== undefined ? ' data-i="' + i + '"' : '') + (j !== undefined ? ' data-j="' + j + '"' : '') + (k !== undefined ? ' data-k="' + k + '"' : ''); }

  /* ----- Academic Guide editor ----- */
  function sectionForm(s) {
    var h = '<div class="pa-form"><div class="pa-head"><b>' + (ui.isNew ? 'New section' : 'Editing section') + '</b><span class="pa-badge">' + (s.type === 'buildings' ? 'Buildings and rooms' : 'Steps and instructions') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="pa-group"><h5>Section details</h5>' +
      fld('Button name <small class="note">(the button at the top of the Academic Guide)</small>', inp('pa-title', s.title)) + fld('Page heading', inp('pa-heading', s.heading)) + fld('Short description', ta('pa-intro', s.intro, 2)) +
      (s.type === 'buildings' ? fld('Search box text', inp('pa-search', s.search)) : '') + '</div>';
    if (s.type === 'buildings') {
      (s.buildings || []).forEach(function (b, i) {
        h += '<div class="pa-group pa-card"><div class="pa-line"><b>Building ' + (i + 1) + '</b><span class="pa-sp"></span>' + pbtn('b-del', 'Remove building', di(i)) + '</div>' + fld('Building name', inp('pa-bn', b.name));
        (b.floors || []).forEach(function (f, j) {
          h += '<div class="pa-sub pa-fl"><div class="pa-line">' + inp('pa-fln', f.label, 'Floor name, e.g. 1st Floor') + '<button type="button" class="pa-x" data-pa="f-del"' + di(i, j) + ' aria-label="Remove this floor" title="Remove this floor">&times;</button></div>';
          (f.rooms || []).forEach(function (r, k) {
            h += '<div class="pa-line pa-rm">' + inp('pa-rt', r.tag, 'No.') + inp('pa-rn', r.name, 'Room or section name') + '<button type="button" class="pa-x" data-pa="r-del"' + di(i, j, k) + ' aria-label="Remove this room" title="Remove this room">&times;</button></div>';
          });
          h += pbtn('r-add', '+ Add room', di(i, j)) + '</div>';
        });
        h += pbtn('f-add', '+ Add floor', di(i)) + '</div>';
      });
      h += pbtn('b-add', '+ Add building');
    } else {
      (s.cards || []).forEach(function (c, i) {
        var n = (s.cards || []).length;
        h += '<div class="pa-group pa-card"><div class="pa-line"><b>Box ' + (i + 1) + '</b><span class="pa-sp"></span>' + pbtn('c-up', '↑', di(i) + (i ? '' : ' disabled') + ' aria-label="Move box up"') + pbtn('c-dn', '↓', di(i) + (i < n - 1 ? '' : ' disabled') + ' aria-label="Move box down"') + pbtn('c-del', 'Remove box', di(i)) + '</div>' + fld('Box title', inp('pa-ct', c.title));
        h += '<label>Steps <small class="note">(numbers are added automatically)</small></label>';
        (c.steps && c.steps.length ? c.steps : ['']).forEach(function (st, j) {
          h += '<div class="pa-line"><span class="pa-num">' + (j + 1) + '</span>' + inp('pa-st', st, 'Describe this step') + '<button type="button" class="pa-x" data-pa="st-del"' + di(i, j) + ' aria-label="Remove this step" title="Remove this step">&times;</button></div>';
        });
        h += pbtn('st-add', '+ Add step', di(i)) + fld('Highlighted note title <small class="note">(optional)</small>', inp('pa-cnt', c.noteTitle, 'e.g. Important')) + fld('Highlighted note text <small class="note">(optional)</small>', ta('pa-cn', c.note, 3)) + '</div>';
      });
      h += pbtn('c-add', '+ Add box') + '<div class="pa-group"><h5>Closing text</h5>' + fld('Text under the boxes <small class="note">(optional)</small>', ta('pa-foot', s.foot, 2)) + '</div>';
    }
    return h + '<div class="row pa-save"><button type="button" class="btn" data-pa="s-save">Save section</button><button type="button" class="btn alt" data-pa="s-cancel">Cancel</button></div></div>';
  }
  function collect() { /* read the form back into the draft so nothing typed is lost; empty rows are kept while editing */
    var s = ui.sec, r = $('#p-root'); if (!s || !r || !$('.pa-title', r)) return;
    var v = function (sel, c) { var e = $(sel, c); return e ? e.value : ''; };
    s.title = v('.pa-title', r); s.heading = v('.pa-heading', r); s.intro = v('.pa-intro', r);
    if (s.type === 'buildings') {
      s.search = v('.pa-search', r);
      s.buildings = $$('.pa-card', r).map(function (c) {
        return { name: v('.pa-bn', c), floors: $$('.pa-fl', c).map(function (f) { return { label: v('.pa-fln', f), rooms: $$('.pa-rm', f).map(function (m) { return { tag: v('.pa-rt', m), name: v('.pa-rn', m) }; }) }; }) };
      });
    } else {
      s.cards = $$('.pa-card', r).map(function (c) { return { title: v('.pa-ct', c), steps: $$('.pa-st', c).map(function (e) { return e.value; }), noteTitle: v('.pa-cnt', c), note: v('.pa-cn', c) }; });
      s.foot = v('.pa-foot', r);
    }
  }
  function cleanSection(s) {
    s.title = s.title.trim(); s.heading = (s.heading || '').trim() || s.title; s.intro = (s.intro || '').trim();
    if (s.type === 'buildings') {
      s.search = (s.search || '').trim();
      s.buildings = (s.buildings || []).map(function (b) {
        return { name: b.name.trim(), floors: (b.floors || []).map(function (f) { return { label: f.label.trim(), rooms: (f.rooms || []).map(function (m) { return { tag: m.tag.trim(), name: m.name.trim() }; }).filter(function (m) { return m.name; }) }; }).filter(function (f) { return f.label || f.rooms.length; }) };
      }).filter(function (b) { return b.name || b.floors.length; });
    } else {
      s.cards = (s.cards || []).map(function (c) { return { title: c.title.trim(), steps: c.steps.map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean), noteTitle: c.noteTitle.trim(), note: c.note.trim() }; }).filter(function (c) { return c.title || c.steps.length; });
      s.foot = (s.foot || '').trim();
    }
    return s;
  }
  function guideHTML(p) {
    if (ui.sec) return sectionForm(ui.sec);
    var h = '<h4 class="chead">Academic Guide</h4><p class="note">Each section below is a button at the top of the public Academic Guide. Adding, renaming, reordering or removing a section updates those buttons right away.</p>';
    if (!p.guide.length) h += '<p class="note empty">No sections yet. Add one below.</p>';
    p.guide.forEach(function (s, i) {
      var k = 'g:' + s.id;
      h += '<div class="crow"><div class="cinfo"><b>' + esc(s.title) + '</b><small>' + (s.type === 'buildings' ? 'Buildings and rooms' : 'Steps and instructions') + '</small></div>' +
        pbtn('g-up', '↑', di(i) + (i ? '' : ' disabled') + ' aria-label="Move up"') + pbtn('g-dn', '↓', di(i) + (i < p.guide.length - 1 ? '' : ' disabled') + ' aria-label="Move down"') +
        pbtn('g-edit', 'Edit', di(i)) + pbtn('g-del', ui.armed === k ? 'Tap again to remove' : 'Remove', di(i), ui.armed === k ? 'bad' : 'alt') + '</div>';
    });
    return h + '<div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn" data-pa="g-new-steps">+ Add instructions section</button><button type="button" class="btn" data-pa="g-new-bld">+ Add buildings section</button></div>';
  }

  /* ----- SBMS (unchanged behaviour: same field, same save) ----- */
  function sbmsHTML(p) {
    return '<h4 class="chead">SBMS button link</h4><p class="note">The SBMS button on the ASHS Portal opens this link.</p>' +
      '<input id="pa-sbms" type="url" inputmode="url" placeholder="https://..." value="' + esc(p.sbms.url || '') + '"><div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn" data-pa="sbms-save">Save link</button></div>';
  }

  /* ----- Persons In Charge ----- */
  function personForm(p) {
    var f = ui.pf, bad = f.id ? descendants(p.persons, f.id) : {}, pic = f.photo && !isPdf(f.photo) && fileOk(f.photo);
    var opts = '<option value="">— Top of the chart (reports to no one) —</option>' + ordered(p.persons).filter(function (o) { return o.x.id !== f.id && !bad[o.x.id]; }).map(function (o) {
      return '<option value="' + esc(o.x.id) + '"' + (o.x.id === f.parent ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + new Array(o.d + 1).join('— ') + esc(o.x.name) + (o.x.role ? ' (' + esc(o.x.role) + ')' : '') + '</option>';
    }).join('');
    return '<div class="pa-form"><div class="pa-head"><b>' + (f.id ? 'Edit person' : 'Add a person') + '</b></div>' +
      '<div class="pa-photo">' + (pic ? '<img class="pa-av big" src="' + esc(furl(f.photo)) + '" alt="Photo preview">' : '<span class="pa-av big oini" aria-hidden="true">' + esc(initials(f.name) || '?') + '</span>') +
      '<div><label for="pa-photofile">Photograph <small class="note">(JPG, PNG or WebP; it is resized automatically)</small></label><input id="pa-photofile" type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp">' + (pic ? pbtn('p-photo-rm', 'Remove photo') : '') + '</div></div>' +
      fld('Name', '<input id="pa-pn" value="' + esc(f.name) + '" placeholder="Full name">') + fld('Official position', '<input id="pa-pr" value="' + esc(f.role) + '" placeholder="e.g. Principal, Assistant Principal, Department Head">') +
      fld('Reports to <small class="note">(this decides the level and the connecting line)</small>', '<select id="pa-pp">' + opts + '</select>') +
      '<div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn" data-pa="p-save">' + (f.id ? 'Save changes' : 'Add person') + '</button><button type="button" class="btn alt" data-pa="p-cancel">Cancel</button></div></div>';
  }
  function personRow(o, p) {
    var x = o.x, k = 'p:' + x.id, sib = p.persons.filter(function (y) { return y.parent === x.parent; }), si = sib.indexOf(x), n = (kidsOf(p.persons)[x.id] || []).length;
    var pic = x.photo && !isPdf(x.photo) && fileOk(x.photo) ? '<img class="pa-av" src="' + esc(furl(x.photo)) + '" alt="">' : '<span class="pa-av oini" aria-hidden="true">' + esc(initials(x.name)) + '</span>';
    return '<div class="crow pa-pr" style="margin-left:' + Math.min(o.d, 5) * 16 + 'px">' + pic + '<div class="cinfo"><b>' + esc(x.name) + '</b><small>' + esc((x.role || 'No position') + (n ? ' · ' + n + ' report' + (n > 1 ? 's' : '') : '')) + '</small></div>' +
      pbtn('p-up', '←', ' data-id="' + esc(x.id) + '"' + (si > 0 ? '' : ' disabled') + ' aria-label="Move earlier (left) among the people at this level" title="Move left"') +
      pbtn('p-dn', '→', ' data-id="' + esc(x.id) + '"' + (si < sib.length - 1 ? '' : ' disabled') + ' aria-label="Move later (right) among the people at this level" title="Move right"') +
      pbtn('p-under', '+ Under', ' data-id="' + esc(x.id) + '" title="Add a person who reports to ' + esc(x.name) + '"') + pbtn('p-edit', 'Edit', ' data-id="' + esc(x.id) + '"') +
      pbtn('p-del', ui.armed === k ? 'Tap again to remove' : 'Remove', ' data-id="' + esc(x.id) + '"', ui.armed === k ? 'bad' : 'alt') + '</div>';
  }
  function personsHTML(p) {
    var org = p.org, hasFile = !!(org.file && fileOk(org.file)), view = ui.pm || org.mode, h = '<h4 class="chead">Persons In Charge · Organizational Chart</h4><p class="note">Choose how the organizational chart is made. The website shows the method marked “Showing on website”. Switching never deletes the other version.</p>';
    h += '<div class="pa-methods">' +
      '<button type="button" class="pa-method' + (view === 'upload' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-pa="pm-upload"><b>Upload Organizational Chart</b><small>Use an existing chart as a PNG, JPG or PDF file.</small>' + (org.mode === 'upload' ? '<span class="pa-live">Showing on website</span>' : '') + '</button>' +
      '<button type="button" class="pa-method' + (view === 'manual' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-pa="pm-manual"><b>Create/Edit Organizational Chart</b><small>Build the chart here with photos, names and positions.</small>' + (org.mode === 'manual' ? '<span class="pa-live">Showing on website</span>' : '') + '</button></div>';
    if (view === 'upload') {
      h += '<div class="pa-group"><h5>Uploaded chart</h5>';
      if (hasFile) {
        h += (isPdf(org.file) ? '<p class="note">PDF file' + (org.name ? ': <b>' + esc(org.name) + '</b>' : '') + '. <a href="' + esc(furl(org.file)) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Open it</a></p>' : '<img class="pa-thumb" src="' + esc(furl(org.file)) + '" alt="Uploaded organizational chart">' + (org.name ? '<p class="note">' + esc(org.name) + '</p>' : '')) +
          '<p class="note">' + (org.mode === 'upload' ? 'This chart is what the website shows now.' : 'This chart is saved but the website currently shows the created chart. Choose “Upload Organizational Chart” above to show it.') + '</p>' +
          '<label for="pa-chartfile">Replace the chart</label><input id="pa-chartfile" type="file" accept=".png,.jpg,.jpeg,.pdf,image/png,image/jpeg,application/pdf"><div class="row">' + pbtn('chart-rm', ui.armed === 'chart' ? 'Tap again to remove the chart' : 'Remove uploaded chart', '', ui.armed === 'chart' ? 'bad' : 'alt') + '</div>';
      } else {
        h += '<p class="note empty">No chart uploaded yet.</p><label for="pa-chartfile">Choose a chart file <small class="note">(PNG, JPG or PDF, up to 8 MB)</small></label><input id="pa-chartfile" type="file" accept=".png,.jpg,.jpeg,.pdf,image/png,image/jpeg,application/pdf">';
      }
      h += '<p class="note">The chart is shown exactly as you upload it. Its boxes and names are part of the picture or PDF, so they cannot be edited here. To change them, upload a new file, or use “Create/Edit Organizational Chart”.</p>' + (ui.busy ? '<p class="note"><b>Uploading…</b></p>' : '') + '</div>';
    } else {
      h += '<div class="pa-group"><h5>People in the chart</h5>';
      if (ui.pf) h += personForm(p);
      else {
        if (!p.persons.length) h += '<p class="note empty">Nobody added yet. Start with the Principal, then use “+ Under” on a person to add the people who report to them.</p>';
        else h += '<p class="note">Indented rows report to the person above them. Use ← → to change the order at the same level, “+ Under” to add someone who reports to that person, or Edit to change who a person reports to.</p>' + ordered(p.persons).map(function (o) { return personRow(o, p); }).join('');
        h += '<div class="row">' + pbtn('p-add', p.persons.length ? '+ Add person at the top level' : '+ Add the first person', '', '') + '</div>';
      }
      h += '</div>';
      if (p.persons.length && !ui.pf) h += '<div class="pa-group"><h5>Preview (as shown on the website)</h5>' + treeHTML(p.persons) + '</div>';
    }
    return h;
  }

  function hubHTML(p) {
    var org = p.org, pc = org.mode === 'upload' && org.file ? 'Uploaded chart is showing' : (p.persons.length ? p.persons.length + (p.persons.length > 1 ? ' people' : ' person') + ' in the chart' : 'No chart yet');
    var sOk = !!safeUrl(p.sbms.url), pOk = org.mode === 'upload' ? !!org.file : p.persons.length > 0;
    function card(act, cls, ico, name, desc, pill, ok) {
      return '<button type="button" class="pa-opt ' + cls + '" data-pa="' + act + '"><span class="pa-ico">' + ico + '</span><span class="pa-txt"><b>' + name + '</b><small>' + desc + '</small></span>' +
        '<span class="pa-foot"><span class="pa-pill' + (ok ? ' ok' : '') + '">' + pill + '</span><span class="pa-go" aria-hidden="true">\u2192</span></span></button>';
    }
    return '<div class="pa-intro"><h4>What would you like to manage?</h4><p class="note">Pick a part of the ASHS Portal to edit.</p></div><div class="pa-hub">' +
      card('hub-guide', 'pa-guide', SVG.guide, 'Academic Guide', 'Enrollment, buildings and other info pages', p.guide.length + ' section' + (p.guide.length === 1 ? '' : 's'), p.guide.length > 0) +
      card('hub-sbms', 'pa-sbms', SVG.sbms, 'SBMS', 'The link students open from the portal', sOk ? 'Link is set' : 'Link not set yet', sOk) +
      card('hub-persons', 'pa-persons', SVG.persons, 'Persons in Charge', 'Organizational chart and photos', pc, pOk) + '</div>';
  }
  function fillAdmin() {
    var r = $('#p-root'); if (!r) return;
    var p = portal();
    if (!ui.pm) ui.pm = p.org.mode;
    r.innerHTML = !ui.hub ? hubHTML(p) : '<button type="button" class="btn alt pa-back" data-pa="hub-back">← Back to Manage ASHS Portal</button>' + (ui.hub === 'guide' ? guideHTML(p) : ui.hub === 'sbms' ? sbmsHTML(p) : personsHTML(p));
    if (ui.focus) { var fn = ui.focus; ui.focus = null; try { var t = fn(r); if (t) t.focus(); } catch (e) { } } /* put the cursor in the row that was just added */
  }
  function arm(k, go) { if (ui.armed === k) { ui.armed = null; go(); } else { ui.armed = k; fillAdmin(); setTimeout(function () { if (ui.armed === k) { ui.armed = null; if (!ui.sec && !ui.pf) fillAdmin(); } }, 4000); } }
  function move(arr, i, d) { var j = i + d; if (j < 0 || j >= arr.length) return; var t = arr[i]; arr[i] = arr[j]; arr[j] = t; }
  function done(p, msg) { if (savePortal(p)) say(msg); else say('Could not save. A photo or other data may be too large, or browser storage is full.'); fillAdmin(); }
  function trash(files) { /* files that are no longer used are removed from the server a moment after the new content is saved */
    var S = window.FIXIT_SYNC, list = (files || []).filter(function (f) { return f && !/^data:/.test(f); });
    if (!list.length || !S || !S.portalDelete) return;
    setTimeout(function () { S.portalDelete({ files: list }); }, 2500);
  }
  function sendFile(kind, data) {
    var S = window.FIXIT_SYNC;
    if (S && S.isOn && S.isOn() && S.portalFile) return S.portalFile({ kind: kind, data: data });
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, file: data, kind: /^data:application\/pdf/.test(data) ? 'pdf' : 'image' }); /* no server (file opened directly): the file is kept in this browser */
  }
  function readFile(file) { return new Promise(function (ok, bad) { var r = new FileReader(); r.onload = function () { ok(String(r.result)); }; r.onerror = function () { bad(); }; r.readAsDataURL(file); }); }
  function loadImg(src) { return new Promise(function (ok, bad) { var im = new Image(); im.onload = function () { ok(im); }; im.onerror = function () { bad(); }; im.src = src; }); }
  function squarePhoto(file) { /* centre-cropped square JPEG, 360 px: small enough to store and sharp enough for a profile card */
    return readFile(file).then(loadImg).then(function (im) {
      var s = Math.min(im.naturalWidth, im.naturalHeight), c = document.createElement('canvas'), n = Math.min(360, s); c.width = c.height = n;
      var g = c.getContext('2d'); g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.fillRect(0, 0, n, n); g.drawImage(im, (im.naturalWidth - s) / 2, (im.naturalHeight - s) / 2, s, s, 0, 0, n, n);
      return c.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85);
    });
  }
  function lastIn(sel, ctx) { return function (r) { var l = $$(sel, ctx ? ctx(r) : r); return l[l.length - 1]; }; }
  function pcollect() { var f = ui.pf; if (!f || !$('#pa-pn')) return; f.name = $('#pa-pn').value; f.role = $('#pa-pr').value; f.parent = $('#pa-pp').value; }
  function newPf(parent) { return { id: '', name: '', role: '', parent: parent || '', photo: '', orig: '' }; }

  function onAdmin(e) {
    var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-pa]'); if (!b || !$('#p-root') || !$('#p-root').contains(b) || b.disabled) return;
    var a = b.getAttribute('data-pa'), i = +b.getAttribute('data-i'), j = +b.getAttribute('data-j'), k = +b.getAttribute('data-k'), id = b.getAttribute('data-id'), p = portal();
    if (a === 'hub-guide' || a === 'hub-sbms' || a === 'hub-persons') { ui.hub = a.slice(4); ui.armed = null; return fillAdmin(); }
    if (a === 'hub-back') { if (ui.pf && ui.pf.photo !== ui.pf.orig) trash([ui.pf.photo]); ui.hub = ''; ui.sec = null; ui.pf = null; ui.armed = null; return fillAdmin(); }
    /* SBMS (unchanged) */
    if (a === 'sbms-save') {
      var raw = $('#pa-sbms').value.trim(), u = safeUrl(raw);
      if (raw && !u) return say('Please enter a full link that starts with http:// or https://');
      p.sbms = { url: u }; return done(p, u ? 'SBMS link saved.' : 'SBMS link cleared.');
    }
    /* Academic Guide */
    if (a === 'g-new-steps' || a === 'g-new-bld') {
      ui.isNew = true; ui.sec = a === 'g-new-bld' ? { id: uid(), type: 'buildings', title: '', heading: '', intro: '', search: 'Search a room or section', buildings: [{ name: '', floors: [] }] }
        : { id: uid(), type: 'steps', title: '', heading: '', intro: '', cards: [{ title: '', steps: [''], noteTitle: '', note: '' }], foot: '' };
      return fillAdmin();
    }
    if (a === 'g-edit') { ui.isNew = false; ui.sec = clone(p.guide[i]); return fillAdmin(); }
    if (a === 'g-del') return arm('g:' + p.guide[i].id, function () { p.guide.splice(i, 1); done(p, 'Section removed. The button is gone from the website.'); });
    if (a === 'g-up' || a === 'g-dn') { move(p.guide, i, a === 'g-up' ? -1 : 1); return done(p, 'Order saved.'); }
    if (ui.sec && /^(c-|st-|b-|f-|r-)/.test(a)) {
      collect(); var s0 = ui.sec;
      if (a === 'c-add') { s0.cards.push({ title: '', steps: [''], noteTitle: '', note: '' }); ui.focus = lastIn('.pa-ct'); }
      if (a === 'c-del') s0.cards.splice(i, 1);
      if (a === 'c-up' || a === 'c-dn') move(s0.cards, i, a === 'c-up' ? -1 : 1);
      if (a === 'st-add') { s0.cards[i].steps.push(''); ui.focus = lastIn('.pa-st', function (r) { return $$('.pa-card', r)[i]; }); }
      if (a === 'st-del') { s0.cards[i].steps.splice(j, 1); if (!s0.cards[i].steps.length) s0.cards[i].steps.push(''); }
      if (a === 'b-add') { s0.buildings.push({ name: '', floors: [] }); ui.focus = lastIn('.pa-bn'); }
      if (a === 'b-del') s0.buildings.splice(i, 1);
      if (a === 'f-add') { s0.buildings[i].floors.push({ label: '', rooms: [] }); ui.focus = lastIn('.pa-fln', function (r) { return $$('.pa-card', r)[i]; }); }
      if (a === 'f-del') s0.buildings[i].floors.splice(j, 1);
      if (a === 'r-add') { s0.buildings[i].floors[j].rooms.push({ tag: '', name: '' }); ui.focus = lastIn('.pa-rt', function (r) { return $$('.pa-fl', $$('.pa-card', r)[i])[j]; }); }
      return fillAdmin();
    }
    if (a === 's-cancel') { ui.sec = null; return fillAdmin(); }
    if (a === 's-save') {
      collect(); var s = ui.sec;
      if (!s.title.trim()) return say('Please enter the button name for this section.');
      s = cleanSection(s);
      var at = -1; p.guide.forEach(function (x, n) { if (x.id === s.id) at = n; });
      if (at > -1) p.guide[at] = s; else p.guide.push(s);
      ui.sec = null; return done(p, 'Section saved. The website buttons are updated.');
    }
    /* Persons In Charge: method */
    if (a === 'pm-upload' || a === 'pm-manual') {
      pcollect(); ui.pm = a === 'pm-upload' ? 'upload' : 'manual';
      if (ui.pm === 'upload' && !(p.org.file && fileOk(p.org.file))) return fillAdmin(); /* nothing uploaded yet: show the uploader; the website changes once a chart is uploaded */
      if (p.org.mode !== ui.pm) { p.org.mode = ui.pm; return done(p, ui.pm === 'upload' ? 'The website now shows the uploaded chart. Your created chart is kept.' : 'The website now shows the created chart. Your uploaded chart is kept.'); }
      return fillAdmin();
    }
    if (a === 'chart-rm') return arm('chart', function () { var old = p.org.file; p.org = { mode: 'manual' }; ui.pm = 'manual'; done(p, 'Uploaded chart removed. The website now shows the created chart.'); trash([old]); });
    /* Persons In Charge: people */
    if (a === 'p-add' || a === 'p-under') { ui.pf = newPf(a === 'p-under' ? id : ''); return fillAdmin(); }
    if (a === 'p-edit') { var x0 = p.persons.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0]; if (!x0) return; ui.pf = { id: x0.id, name: x0.name, role: x0.role || '', parent: x0.parent || '', photo: x0.photo || '', orig: x0.photo || '' }; return fillAdmin(); }
    if (a === 'p-cancel') { if (ui.pf && ui.pf.photo !== ui.pf.orig) trash([ui.pf.photo]); ui.pf = null; return fillAdmin(); }
    if (a === 'p-photo-rm') { pcollect(); if (ui.pf.photo !== ui.pf.orig) trash([ui.pf.photo]); ui.pf.photo = ''; return fillAdmin(); }
    if (a === 'p-del') return arm('p:' + id, function () {
      var x = p.persons.filter(function (y) { return y.id === id; })[0]; if (!x) return;
      p.persons.forEach(function (y) { if (y.parent === id) y.parent = x.parent || ''; }); /* the team moves up to the removed person's own boss */
      p.persons = p.persons.filter(function (y) { return y.id !== id; }); relevel(p.persons);
      done(p, 'Person removed.'); trash([x.photo]);
    });
    if (a === 'p-up' || a === 'p-dn') {
      var me = p.persons.filter(function (y) { return y.id === id; })[0]; if (!me) return;
      var sib = p.persons.filter(function (y) { return y.parent === me.parent; }), si = sib.indexOf(me), ot = sib[si + (a === 'p-up' ? -1 : 1)]; if (!ot) return;
      var ia = p.persons.indexOf(me), ib = p.persons.indexOf(ot); p.persons[ia] = ot; p.persons[ib] = me;
      return done(p, 'Order saved.');
    }
    if (a === 'p-save') {
      pcollect(); var f = ui.pf, name = f.name.trim(), role = f.role.trim();
      if (!name) return say('Please enter the name.');
      if (f.id && (f.parent === f.id || descendants(p.persons, f.id)[f.parent])) return say('A person cannot report to someone on their own team.');
      var trashed = [];
      if (f.id) p.persons.forEach(function (x) { if (x.id === f.id) { x.name = name; x.role = role; x.parent = f.parent; if (f.photo) x.photo = f.photo; else delete x.photo; if (f.orig && f.orig !== f.photo) trashed.push(f.orig); } });
      else { var np = { id: uid(), name: name, role: role, parent: f.parent, level: 1 }; if (f.photo) np.photo = f.photo; p.persons.push(np); }
      relevel(p.persons); ui.pf = null; done(p, 'Saved. The organizational chart is updated.'); trash(trashed);
    }
  }
  function onFile(e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.files || !$('#p-root') || !$('#p-root').contains(t)) return;
    var file = t.files[0]; if (!file) return; t.value = '';
    if (t.id === 'pa-photofile') {
      if (!/^image\/(png|jpe?g|webp)$/i.test(file.type) && !/\.(png|jpe?g|webp)$/i.test(file.name)) return say('Please choose a JPG, PNG or WebP photo.');
      pcollect(); say('Preparing photo…');
      squarePhoto(file).then(function (data) { return sendFile('photo', data); }).then(function (j) {
        if (!j || !j.ok) return say((j && j.error) || 'The photo could not be uploaded.');
        if (ui.pf && ui.pf.photo !== ui.pf.orig) trash([ui.pf.photo]);
        if (ui.pf) ui.pf.photo = j.file; fillAdmin(); say('Photo ready. Press Save to keep it.');
      }).catch(function () { say('That photo could not be read. Please choose another image.'); });
    }
    if (t.id === 'pa-chartfile') {
      var okType = /\.(png|jpe?g|pdf)$/i.test(file.name) && (!file.type || /^(image\/(png|jpeg)|application\/pdf)$/i.test(file.type));
      if (!okType) return say('Please choose a PNG, JPG or PDF file.');
      if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) return say('That file is too large. The maximum is 8 MB.');
      if (file.size < 100) return say('That file looks empty.');
      var cur = portal().org;
      if (cur.file && !window.confirm('Replace the current organizational chart with “' + file.name + '”?')) return;
      ui.busy = true; fillAdmin();
      readFile(file).then(function (data) { return /\.pdf$/i.test(file.name) ? data : loadImg(data).then(function () { return data; }); })
        .then(function (data) { return sendFile('chart', data); })
        .then(function (j) {
          ui.busy = false;
          if (!j || !j.ok) { fillAdmin(); return say((j && j.error) || 'The chart could not be uploaded.'); }
          var p = portal(), old = p.org.file;
          p.org = { mode: 'upload', file: j.file, kind: j.kind, name: file.name.slice(0, 120) }; ui.pm = 'upload';
          done(p, 'Chart uploaded. The website now shows it.'); if (old && old !== j.file) trash([old]);
        }).catch(function () { ui.busy = false; fillAdmin(); say('That file could not be read as an image or PDF.'); });
    }
  }

  window.FIXIT_PORTAL = { isDirty: function () { return !!(ui.sec || ui.pf); }, reset: function () { if (ui.pf && ui.pf.photo !== ui.pf.orig) trash([ui.pf.photo]); ui.hub = ''; ui.sec = null; ui.pf = null; ui.pm = ''; ui.armed = null; ui.busy = false; fillAdmin(); }, mountAdmin: function () { ui.hub = ''; ui.sec = null; ui.pf = null; ui.pm = ''; ui.armed = null; ui.busy = false; fillAdmin(); }, render: renderPublic };
  var h0 = window.FIXIT_HOME && window.FIXIT_HOME.render;
  if (h0) window.FIXIT_HOME.render = function () { h0.apply(this, arguments); renderPublic(); }; /* homepage content changed (here or on the server): refresh the portal pages too */
  wire(); document.addEventListener('click', onAdmin); document.addEventListener('change', onFile); renderPublic();
})();
